"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Layers } from "lucide-react";
import { Shimmer , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";

type CohortRow = {
  cohort: string;
  size: number;
  weeks: number[];
};

const COHORT_DATA: CohortRow[] = [
  { cohort: "Jun 2026", size: 420, weeks: [100, 68, 54, 47, 41, 38] },
  { cohort: "Jul 2026", size: 512, weeks: [100, 71, 58, 50, 44, 0] },
  { cohort: "Aug 2026", size: 389, weeks: [100, 74, 61, 0, 0, 0] }
];

function heatColor(pct: number) {
  if (pct === 0) return "transparent";
  const alpha = Math.max(0.08, pct / 100);
  return `rgba(20, 23, 26, ${alpha})`;
}

export default function CohortAnalysisPage() {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIsLoading(false), 600);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <Layers size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Cohort Analysis</h1><HelpTooltip text="Shows what percentage of each signup cohort remains active week over week. Higher retention across later weeks means learners keep coming back." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Weekly retention percentage by learner signup cohort.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
            <Shimmer key={i} className="h-14 w-full rounded-control" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[640px] border-collapse text-[13px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-line text-left text-ink-soft">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Cohort</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Size</th>
                {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
                  <th key={i} className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Wk {i}</th>
                ))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {COHORT_DATA.map((row) => (
                <tr key={row.cohort} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-ink">{row.cohort}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 text-ink-soft">{row.size}</td>
                  {row.weeks.map((pct, i) => (
                    <td key={i} className="px-4 py-3">
                      {pct > 0 ? (
                        <span
                          className="inline-flex h-8 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-[12px] font-medium text-ink"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: heatColor(pct) }}
                        >
                          {pct}%
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-ink-soft/40">-</span>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { DollarSign, Repeat, GraduationCap, Undo2 } from "lucide-react";
import { LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, CartesianGrid } from "recharts";
import { KpiCard, ShimmerKpiCard, ShimmerChart , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency } from "@saachi/utils";

const GMV_TREND = [
  { month: "Mar", gmv: 18400 },
  { month: "Apr", gmv: 21200 },
  { month: "May", gmv: 24900 },
  { month: "Jun", gmv: 27600 },
  { month: "Jul", gmv: 31800 },
  { month: "Aug", gmv: 35200 }
];

export default function AnalyticsOverviewPage() {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIsLoading(false), 600);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  const kpis = [
    { label: "Gross Merchandise Value", value: formatCurrency(35200, "USD"), icon: DollarSign, delta: { value: "+10.7%", direction: "up" as const } },
    { label: "Subscription MRR", value: formatCurrency(6840, "USD"), icon: Repeat, delta: { value: "+4.2%", direction: "up" as const } },
    { label: "Completion rate", value: "62%", icon: GraduationCap, delta: { value: "+3pts", direction: "up" as const } },
    { label: "Refund rate", value: "2.4%", icon: Undo2, delta: { value: "-0.3pts", direction: "down" as const } }
  ];

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Analytics Overview</h1><HelpTooltip text="Platform-wide financial and engagement metrics: Gross Merchandise Value, subscription revenue, course completion, and refund rates." /></div>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Platform-wide GMV, subscription revenue, completion, and refund metrics.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {isLoading
          ? Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />)
          : kpis.map((kpi) => <KpiCard key={kpi.label} {...kpi} />)}
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <ShimmerChart />
      ) : (
        <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <div className="mb-1 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">GMV - last 6 months</div>
          <p className="mb-5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Gross transaction value across all courses and subscriptions.</p>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={260}>
            <LineChart data={GMV_TREND}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="var(--color-line)" />
              <XAxis dataKey="month" stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
              <YAxis stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
              <Tooltip
                contentStyle={{
                  background: "var(--color-card)",
                  border: "1px solid var(--color-line)",
                  borderRadius: 10,
                  fontSize: 12,
                  color: "var(--color-ink)"
                }}
              />
              <Line type="monotone" dataKey="gmv" stroke="var(--color-ink)" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            </LineChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\courses\catalog\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Search, LibraryBig, Star, Pin } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import {
  useAdminCourseCatalog,
  useAdminCategoryList,
  useToggleCourseFlag,
  type AdminCourse
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { DataTable, StatusPill, Select , RefreshButton , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Draft", value: "draft" },
  { label: "Pending review", value: "pending_review" },
  { label: "Published", value: "published" },
  { label: "Rejected", value: "rejected" },
  { label: "Archived", value: "archived" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminCourse["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info" | "neutral"> = {
  draft: "neutral",
  pending_review: "warning",
  published: "success",
  rejected: "error",
  archived: "info"
};

export default function CourseCatalogPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");

  const { data: categories } = useAdminCategoryList();
  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminCourseCatalog({
    search: search || undefined,
    categoryId: categoryId || undefined,
    status: status || undefined
  });
  const toggleFlag = useToggleCourseFlag();

  const categoryOptions = useMemo(
    () => (categories ?? []).map((c) => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id })),
    [categories]
  );

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<AdminCourse, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Course",
        accessorKey: "title",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="h-9 w-16 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim">
              {row.original.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={row.original.thumbnailUrl} alt={row.original.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="max-w-[260px]">
              <div className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.title}</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.instructorName}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Category",
        accessorKey: "categoryId",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Price",
        accessorKey: "price",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">
            {formatCurrency(row.original.price, row.original.currency)}
          </span>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as AdminCourse["status"];
          return <StatusPill label={value.replace("_", " ")} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Enrollments",
        accessorKey: "enrollmentCount",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="font-mono text-ink-soft">{(getValue() as number).toLocaleString()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Submitted",
        accessorKey: "submittedAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Curate",
        id: "curate",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                toggleFlag.mutate({ id: row.original.id, field: "featured" });
              }}
              title="Toggle featured"
              className={`flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-control border transition-colors duration-150 ease-out ${
                row.original.featured ? "border-ink bg-ink text-paper" : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:text-ink"
              }`}
            >
              <Star size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                toggleFlag.mutate({ id: row.original.id, field: "pinned" });
              }}
              title="Toggle pinned"
              className={`flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-control border transition-colors duration-150 ease-out ${
                row.original.pinned ? "border-ink bg-ink text-paper" : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:text-ink"
              }`}
            >
              <Pin size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )
      }
    ],
    [toggleFlag]
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <LibraryBig size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Course Catalog</h1><HelpTooltip text="Every course on the platform regardless of status. Use the star and pin icons to feature or pin a course for homepage curation." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Full catalog across every status - feature or pin courses for curation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mb-3 flex justify-end"><RefreshButton /></div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        emptyTitle="No courses found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by title or instructor"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[220px]">
              <Select placeholder="All categories" options={categoryOptions} value={categoryId} onChange={(e) => setCategoryId(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\courses\categories\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Tags, Plus, Pencil, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import {
  useAdminCategoryList,
  useCreateCategory,
  useUpdateCategory,
  useDeleteCategory,
  type AdminCategory
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Modal, ShimmerTableRow, Badge , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function CategoryManagementPage() {
  const { data: categories, isLoading } = useAdminCategoryList();
  const createCategory = useCreateCategory();
  const updateCategory = useUpdateCategory();
  const deleteCategory = useDeleteCategory();

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AdminCategory | null>(null);
  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [tagInput, setTagInput] = useState("");
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<AdminCategory | null>(null);

  const openCreate = () => {
    setName("");
    setTags([]);
    setTagInput("");
    setShowCreate(true);
  };

  const openEdit = (category: AdminCategory) => {
    setEditing(category);
    setName(category.name);
    setTags(category.tags);
    setTagInput("");
  };

  const addTag = () => {
    const value = tagInput.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!value || tags.includes(value)) return;
    setTags((prev) => [...prev, value]);
    setTagInput("");
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!name.trim()) return;
    if (editing) {
      updateCategory.mutate({ id: editing.id, name: name.trim(), tags });
      setEditing(null);
    } else {
      createCategory.mutate({ name: name.trim(), tags });
      setShowCreate(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    deleteCategory.mutate(deleteTarget.id);
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  };

  const modalOpen = showCreate || Boolean(editing);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Tags size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Category & Tag Management</h1><HelpTooltip text="The taxonomy used across the marketplace and course builder. Deleting a category will require recategorizing any courses assigned to it." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Taxonomy used across the marketplace and course builder.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={openCreate}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New category
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
        {isLoading ? (
          Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={3} />)
        ) : (
          categories?.map((category) => (
            <div key={category.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-line px-5 py-4 last:border-0 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{category.name}</p>
                  <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">/{category.slug}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {category.tags.map((tag) => (
                    <Badge key={tag}>{tag}</Badge>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                <span className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{category.courseCount} courses</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => openEdit(category)}
                    className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                  >
                    <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(category)}
                    className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      <Modal
        open={modalOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          setShowCreate(false);
          setEditing(null);
        }}
        title={editing ? "Edit category" : "New category"}
        footer={
          <>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowCreate(false);
                setEditing(null);
              }}
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSave} disabled={!name.trim() || createCategory.isPending || updateCategory.isPending}>
              {editing ? "Save changes" : "Create category"}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Category name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. AI for Careers & Job Search" />
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">Tags</label>
            <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {tags.map((tag) => (
                <span key={tag} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line-strong px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] text-ink">
                  {tag}
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => setTags((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t !== tag))} className="text-ink-soft hover:text-error">
                    <X size={11} strokeWidth={2} />
                  </button>
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Input
                placeholder="Add a tag and press Enter"
                value={tagInput}
                onChange={(e) => setTagInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === "Enter") {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    addTag();
                  }
                }}
                className="h-10 flex-1"
              />
              <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={addTag}>
                Add
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(deleteTarget)}
        onClose={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
        title="Delete category?"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleDelete} disabled={deleteCategory.isPending}>
              Delete
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          This will remove <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{deleteTarget?.name}</span> from the taxonomy.
          Courses already assigned to it will need to be recategorized.
        </p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\courses\moderation\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, ClipboardCheck, User, Tag } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminModerationQueue, useModerateCourse, type AdminCourse } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Textarea, Modal, EmptyState, ShimmerKpiCard , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

export default function CourseModerationQueuePage() {
  const { data: queue, isLoading } = useAdminModerationQueue();
  const moderate = useModerateCourse();
  const [rejectTarget, setRejectTarget] = useState<AdminCourse | null>(null);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");

  const handleApprove = (course: AdminCourse) => {
    moderate.mutate({ id: course.id, decision: "published" });
  };

  const handleRejectSubmit = () => {
    if (!rejectTarget) return;
    moderate.mutate({ id: rejectTarget.id, decision: "rejected", reason });
    setRejectTarget(null);
    setReason("");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <ClipboardCheck size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Course Moderation Queue</h1><HelpTooltip text="Courses submitted by instructors that are waiting for approval before they go live. Approve to publish, or reject with feedback so the instructor can revise." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Review newly submitted courses before they go live.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !queue || queue.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<ClipboardCheck size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          title="Queue is clear"
          description="No courses are currently pending review."
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {queue.map((course) => (
            <Card key={course.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <div className="h-[90px] w-[160px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim">
                {course.thumbnailUrl && (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img src={course.thumbnailUrl} alt={course.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1">
                <h3 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{course.title}</h3>
                <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <User size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> {course.instructorName}
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Tag size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> {course.categoryId}
                  </span>
                  <span>Submitted {formatDate(course.submittedAt)}</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">
                    {formatCurrency(course.price, course.currency)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-2">
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setRejectTarget(course)}>
                  <XCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Reject
                </Button>
                <Button size="sm" onClick={() => handleApprove(course)} disabled={moderate.isPending}>
                  <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Approve
                </Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(rejectTarget)}
        onClose={() => setRejectTarget(null)}
        title="Reject course submission"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRejectTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleRejectSubmit} disabled={!reason.trim() || moderate.isPending}>
              {moderate.isPending ? "Sending..." : "Send feedback & reject"}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Rejecting <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{rejectTarget?.title}</span>. This feedback is sent to
          the instructor so they can revise and resubmit.
        </p>
        <Textarea
          label="Feedback for instructor"
          placeholder="Explain what needs to change before this course can be approved..."
          rows={5}
          value={reason}
          onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
        />
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search, History, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { DataTable, Select, StatusPill , RefreshButton , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatRelativeTime } from "@saachi/utils";

type ActivityRow = {
  id: string;
  type: "transaction" | "course" | "ticket" | "user";
  description: string;
  actor: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

const TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Transaction", value: "transaction" },
  { label: "Course", value: "course" },
  { label: "Ticket", value: "ticket" },
  { label: "User", value: "user" }
];

const TYPE_TONE: Record<ActivityRow["type"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info" | "neutral"> = {
  transaction: "success",
  course: "info",
  ticket: "warning",
  user: "neutral"
};

const ACTIVITY_ROUTE: Record<ActivityRow["type"], string> = {
  transaction: "/finance/transactions",
  course: "/courses/moderation",
  ticket: "/support/tickets",
  user: "/users"
};

const ACTORS = ["Fatima Noor", "Hassan Raza", "Ali Bukhari", "Sana Iqbal", "Zeeshan Tariq", "Ayesha Malik", "Bilal Ahmed", "Nida Khan"];

const DESCRIPTIONS: Record<ActivityRow["type"], string[]> = {
  transaction: [
    'Purchased "AI Automation with n8n"',
    'Purchased "Prompt Engineering for Marketers"',
    'Purchased "AI Customer Support Bots"',
    "Upgraded to Saachi AI Pro subscription"
  ],
  course: [
    "Submitted a new course for review",
    "Resubmitted course after feedback",
    "Published a free intro lesson"
  ],
  ticket: [
    "Opened ticket - payout not received",
    "Opened ticket - certificate not generated",
    "Opened ticket - refund status inquiry"
  ],
  user: [
    "New instructor application submitted",
    "New learner account registered",
    "Requested account verification"
  ]
};

function buildMockActivity(count: number): ActivityRow[] {
  const types: ActivityRow["type"][] = ["transaction", "course", "ticket", "user"];
  const rows: ActivityRow[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const type = types[i % types.length];
    const descPool = DESCRIPTIONS[type];
    rows.push({
      id: `act_${i + 1}`,
      type,
      description: descPool[i % descPool.length],
      actor: ACTORS[i % ACTORS.length],
      createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - i * 3.7 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()
    });
  }
  return rows;
}

const ALL_ACTIVITY = buildMockActivity(64);

export default function DashboardActivityPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState("");

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return ALL_ACTIVITY.filter((row) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        !search ||
        row.description.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        row.actor.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
      const matchesType = !typeFilter || row.type === typeFilter;
      return matchesSearch && matchesType;
    });
  }, [search, typeFilter]);

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<ActivityRow, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Activity",
        accessorKey: "description",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="max-w-[360px]">
            <div className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.description}</div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.actor}</div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Type",
        accessorKey: "type",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as ActivityRow["type"];
          return <StatusPill label={value} tone={TYPE_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "When",
        accessorKey: "createdAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatRelativeTime(getValue() as string)}</span>
      }
    ],
    []
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => router.push("/dashboard")}
        className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:text-ink"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Back to dashboard
      </button>

      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <History size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Recent Activity</h1><HelpTooltip text="The full activity log across transactions, course submissions, support tickets, and new user signups." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Full activity log across transactions, courses, tickets, and users.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mb-3 flex justify-end"><RefreshButton /></div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={filtered}
        isLoading={false}
        emptyTitle="No activity found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        onRowClick={(row: ActivityRow) => router.push(ACTIVITY_ROUTE[row.type])}
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by description or actor"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All types" options={TYPE_OPTIONS} value={typeFilter} onChange={(e) => setTypeFilter(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [pieView, setPieView] = useState<PieView>("revenue");
  const [dateRange, setDateRange] = useState<DateRange>("7d");
  const [openHelp, setOpenHelp] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notifTarget, setNotifTarget] = useState<NotificationItem | null>(null);
  const [actionTarget, setActionTarget] = useState<QuickAction | null>(null);
  const [statusTarget, setStatusTarget] = useState<(typeof SYSTEM_STATUS)[number] | null>(null);
  const [currentRole, setCurrentRoleState] = useState<StaffRole>("super_admin");
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentRoleState(getCurrentRole());
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIsLoading(false), 700);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLoading) return;
    const id = toast("Dashboard data refreshed", {
      description: "KPIs, charts, and activity are up to date as of just now.",
      duration: 5000
    });
    return () => {
      toast.dismiss(id);
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [isLoading]);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside() {
      setOpenHelp(null);
    }
    if (openHelp) {
      document.addEventListener("click", handleClickOutside);
    }
    return () => document.removeEventListener("click", handleClickOutside);
  }, [openHelp]);

  const allKpis: KpiDef[] = [
    {
      key: "signups",
      label: "New signups today",
      value: "142",
      icon: Users,
      delta: { value: "+12%", direction: "up" },
      href: "/users",

      href: "/support/tickets",
      help: "Support tickets that are still open or in progress and awaiting resolution."
    }
  ];

  // ---- Role-aware filtering (single source of truth: lib/permissions.ts) ----
  const kpis = useMemo(
    () => (mounted ? allKpis.filter((k) => canAccessModule(KPI_MODULE_MAP[k.key], currentRole)) : allKpis),
    [mounted, currentRole]
  );

  const quickActions = useMemo(
    () =>
      mounted
        ? QUICK_ACTIONS.filter((a) => canAccessModule(QUICK_ACTION_MODULE_MAP[a.id], currentRole))
        : QUICK_ACTIONS,
    [mounted, currentRole]
  );

  const notifications = useMemo(
    () =>
      mounted
        ? NOTIFICATIONS.filter((n) => canAccessModule(NOTIFICATION_MODULE_MAP[n.id], currentRole))
        : NOTIFICATIONS,
    [mounted, currentRole]
  );

  const recentActivity = useMemo(
    () =>
      mounted
        ? RECENT_ACTIVITY.filter((a) => canAccessModule(ACTIVITY_TYPE_MODULE_MAP[a.type], currentRole))
        : RECENT_ACTIVITY,
    [mounted, currentRole]
  );

  const showRevenueChart = !mounted || canAccessModule(REVENUE_CHART_MODULE, currentRole);
  const showPieChart = !mounted || canAccessModule(PIE_CHART_MODULE, currentRole);
  const showEnrollmentChart = !mounted || canAccessModule(ENROLLMENT_CHART_MODULE, currentRole);
  const showSystemStatus = !mounted || canAccessModule("/settings/commission-tiers", currentRole);

  const hasAnyWidgets =
    kpis.length > 0 ||
    quickActions.length > 0 ||
    showRevenueChart ||
    showPieChart ||
    showEnrollmentChart ||
    notifications.length > 0 ||
    recentActivity.length > 0;

  const activityColumns = useMemo<ColumnDef<ActivityRow, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Activity",
        accessorKey: "description",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="max-w-[320px]">
            <div className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.description}</div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.actor}</div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Type",
        accessorKey: "type",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="capitalize text-ink-soft">{getValue() as string}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "When",
        accessorKey: "createdAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatRelativeTime(getValue() as string)}</span>
      }
    ],
    []
  );

  const activePie = PIE_DATASETS[pieView];
  const activeRevenue = REVENUE_TREND[dateRange];

  const handleActionConfirm = () => {
    if (!actionTarget) return;
    if (actionTarget.id === "export_report") {
      toast.success("Report export started", { description: "Your CSV will download shortly." });
    } else if (actionTarget.href) {
      router.push(actionTarget.href);
    }
    setActionTarget(null);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[24px] font-semibold text-ink">Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Platform-wide overview and pending operational items.</p>
        </div>

        {showRevenueChart && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control border border-line bg-card p-1">
            <Calendar size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} className="ml-1.5 text-ink-soft" />
            {(Object.keys(RANGE_LABEL) as DateRange[]).map((range) => (
              <button
                key={range}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDateRange(range)}
                className={`rounded-control px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out ${
                  dateRange === range ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                }`}
              >
                {RANGE_LABEL[range]}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* System status strip - Super Admin only (ties to platform-wide config access) */}
      {showSystemStatus && (
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line bg-card px-4 py-2.5">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">
            <Activity size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> System status
          </span>
          <span className="h-4 w-px bg-line" />
          {SYSTEM_STATUS.map((s) => (
            <button
              key={s.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStatusTarget(s)}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line px-2.5 py-1 text-[11.5px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-line-strong hover:text-ink"
            >
              <span className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${s.ok ? "bg-success" : "bg-error"}`} />
              {s.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* KPI cards - filtered by role's accessible modules */}
      {(isLoading || kpis.length > 0) && (
        <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {isLoading
            ? Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />)
            : kpis.map((kpi) => (
                <div
                  key={kpi.key}
                  role="button"
                  tabIndex={0}
                  onClick={() => router.push(kpi.href)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === "Enter") router.push(kpi.href);
                  }}
                  className="group relative cursor-pointer rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5 transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-line-strong"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                    <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
                      <kpi.icon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          setOpenHelp((prev) => (prev === kpi.key ? null : kpi.key));
                        }}
                        className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                        aria-label={`What is ${kpi.label}?`}
                      >
                        <HelpCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                      </button>
                      <ArrowUpRight
                        size={14}
                        strokeWidth={1.75}
                        className="text-ink-soft opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-150 ease-out group-hover:opacity-100"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <p className="mt-3.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{kpi.label}</p>
                  <div className="mt-1 flex items-end justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{kpi.value}</span>
                    <span
                      className={`mb-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium ${
                        kpi.delta.direction === "up" ? "text-success" : "text-error"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {kpi.delta.value}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {openHelp === kpi.key && (
                    <div
                      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                      className="absolute left-0 top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-20 w-[240px] rounded-control border border-line bg-card p-3 text-[12px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft shadow-lg"
                    >
                      {kpi.help}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Quick actions row - filtered by role's accessible modules */}
      {(isLoading || quickActions.length > 0) && (
        <div className="mb-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <div className="mb-4 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Quick actions</div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
            {(isLoading ? QUICK_ACTIONS : quickActions).map((action) => (
              <button
                key={action.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActionTarget(action)}
                className="flex flex-col items-start gap-2.5 rounded-control border border-line p-4 text-left transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-line-strong hover:bg-paper-dim"
              >
                <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
                  <action.icon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </div>
                <span className="text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{action.label}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Line chart + Pie chart - both gated behind /finance access */}
      {(showRevenueChart || showPieChart) && (
        <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.3fr_1fr]">
          {showRevenueChart &&
            (isLoading ? (
              <ShimmerChart />
            ) : (
              <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
                <div className="mb-1 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Revenue - {RANGE_LABEL[dateRange].toLowerCase()}</div>
                <p className="mb-5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Gross transaction volume across all payment gateways.</p>
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
                  <LineChart data={activeRevenue}>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="var(--color-line)" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="day" stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                    <YAxis stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                    <Tooltip
                      contentStyle={{
                        background: "var(--color-card)",
                        border: "1px solid var(--color-line)",
                        borderRadius: 10,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: "var(--color-ink)"
                      }}
                    />
                    <Line type="monotone" dataKey="revenue" stroke="var(--color-ink)" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
                  </LineChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            ))}

          {showPieChart &&
            (isLoading ? (
              <ShimmerChart />
            ) : (
              <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
                <div className="mb-1 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{activePie.label}</div>
                <p className="mb-4 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Tap a view below to switch what the chart shows.</p>
                <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {(Object.keys(PIE_DATASETS) as PieView[]).map((key) => (
                    <button
                      key={key}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setPieView(key)}
                      className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-[11.5px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out ${
                        pieView === key
                          ? "border-ink bg-ink text-paper"
                          : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:border-line-strong hover:text-ink"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {PIE_DATASETS[key].label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie data={activePie.data} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" innerRadius={48} outerRadius={78} paddingAngle={2}>
                      {activePie.data.map((_, i) => (
                        <Cell key={i} fill={PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length]} />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>
                    <Tooltip
                      contentStyle={{
                        background: "var(--color-card)",
                        border: "1px solid var(--color-line)",
                        borderRadius: 10,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: "var(--color-ink)"
                      }}
                    />
                    <Legend wrapperStyle={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Bar chart - enrollments by category (analytics access) */}
      {showEnrollmentChart &&
        (isLoading ? (
          <div className="mb-8">
            <ShimmerChart />
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mb-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
            <div className="mb-1 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Enrollments by category</div>
            <p className="mb-5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">New course enrollments this period, grouped by top-level category.</p>
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
              <BarChart data={ENROLLMENTS_BY_CATEGORY}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="var(--color-line)" vertical={false} />
                <XAxis dataKey="category" stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={11} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                <YAxis stroke="var(--color-ink-soft)" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                <Tooltip
                  cursor={{ fill: "var(--color-paper-dim)" }}
                  contentStyle={{
                    background: "var(--color-card)",
                    border: "1px solid var(--color-line)",
                    borderRadius: 10,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    color: "var(--color-ink)"
                  }}
                />
                <Bar dataKey="enrollments" fill="var(--color-ink)" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} maxBarSize={48} />
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        ))}

      {/* Notifications card - filtered by role's accessible modules */}
      {(isLoading || notifications.length > 0) && (
        <div className="mb-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Bell size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="text-ink" />
              <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Notifications</div>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[11.5px] text-ink-soft">{notifications.length} active</span>
          </div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
                <Shimmer key={i} className="h-11 w-full" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              {notifications.map((n) => (
                <button
                  key={n.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setNotifTarget(n)}
                  className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-control border border-line px-3.5 py-3 text-left text-[13px] text-ink transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <span className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full ${TONE_DOT[n.tone]}`} />
                    <span className="text-[12.5px]">{n.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  <ChevronRight size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Recent activity table - filtered by role's accessible activity types */}
      {(isLoading || recentActivity.length > 0) && (
        <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Recent activity</div>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Latest activity across the modules you have access to.</p>
            </div>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => router.push("/dashboard/activity")}>
              View more
            </Button>
          </div>

          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                <Shimmer key={i} className="h-11 w-full" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <DataTable
              columns={activityColumns}
              data={recentActivity}
              isLoading={false}
              emptyTitle="No recent activity"
              emptyDescription=""
              onRowClick={(row: ActivityRow) => router.push(ACTIVITY_ROUTE[row.type])}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Edge case: role has no accessible dashboard widgets at all */}
      {!isLoading && !hasAnyWidgets && (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<LifeBuoy size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          title="No dashboard widgets available"
          description="Your current role does not have access to any dashboard modules. Use the sidebar to navigate to a page you have permission for."
        />
      )}

      <Modal open={Boolean(notifTarget)} onClose={() => setNotifTarget(null)} title={notifTarget?.label ?? ""}>
        <p className="mb-5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{notifTarget?.detail}</p>
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setNotifTarget(null)}>
            Close
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              if (notifTarget) router.push(notifTarget.href);
              setNotifTarget(null);
            }}
          >
            Go to related page
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal open={Boolean(actionTarget)} onClose={() => setActionTarget(null)} title={actionTarget?.label ?? ""}>
        <p className="mb-5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{actionTarget?.description}</p>
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setActionTarget(null)}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={handleActionConfirm}>{actionTarget?.confirmLabel}</Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal open={Boolean(statusTarget)} onClose={() => setStatusTarget(null)} title={statusTarget?.label ?? ""}>
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${statusTarget?.ok ? "bg-success" : "bg-error"}`} />
          <span className="text-[13px] font-medium text-ink">{statusTarget?.ok ? "Operational" : "Degraded"}</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{statusTarget?.detail}</p>
        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end">
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setStatusTarget(null)}>
            Close
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\finance\refunds\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Undo2, CheckCircle2, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminRefunds, useResolveRefund, type AdminRefund } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Select, StatusPill, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState, Modal , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Approved", value: "approved" },
  { label: "Rejected", value: "rejected" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminRefund["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error"> = {
  pending: "warning",
  approved: "success",
  rejected: "error"
};

export default function RefundManagementPage() {
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("");
  const { data: refunds, isLoading } = useAdminRefunds({ status: statusFilter || undefined });
  const resolveRefund = useResolveRefund();
  const [viewTarget, setViewTarget] = useState<AdminRefund | null>(null);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Undo2 size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Refund Management</h1><HelpTooltip text="Learner-initiated refund requests. Approving a refund reverses the transaction and claws back the instructor's earned balance." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Learner refund requests - approving reverses the transaction and clawbacks instructor earnings.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="w-[180px]">
          <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !refunds || refunds.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Undo2 size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No refund requests found" description="Try a different status filter." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {refunds.map((refund) => (
            <Card key={refund.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="truncate text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{refund.courseTitle}</p>
                  <StatusPill label={refund.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[refund.status]} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                  {refund.learnerName} - Requested {formatDate(refund.requestedAt)} - {refund.transactionId}
                </p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setViewTarget(refund)}
                  className="mt-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-ink underline decoration-line-strong underline-offset-2 hover:decoration-ink"
                >
                  View reason
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                <span className="font-mono text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {formatCurrency(refund.amount, refund.currency)}
                </span>
                {refund.status === "pending" && (
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolveRefund.mutate({ id: refund.id, status: "rejected" })}>
                      <XCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Reject
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="sm" onClick={() => resolveRefund.mutate({ id: refund.id, status: "approved" })}>
                      <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Approve
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal open={Boolean(viewTarget)} onClose={() => setViewTarget(null)} title="Refund reason">
        <p className="mb-1 text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{viewTarget?.courseTitle}</p>
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">{viewTarget?.reason}</p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\finance\tax-settings\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Percent, Plus, Pencil, Check, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminTaxRules, useUpdateTaxRule, useCreateTaxRule, type TaxRule } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Modal, ShimmerTableRow , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function TaxSettingsPage() {
  const { data: rules, isLoading } = useAdminTaxRules();
  const updateRule = useUpdateTaxRule();
  const createRule = useCreateTaxRule();

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<TaxRule | null>(null);
  const [editRate, setEditRate] = useState(0);
  const [editActive, setEditActive] = useState(true);

  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [newRegion, setNewRegion] = useState("");
  const [newRate, setNewRate] = useState(0);
  const [newAppliesTo, setNewAppliesTo] = useState("");

  const openEdit = (rule: TaxRule) => {
    setEditing(rule);
    setEditRate(rule.ratePercent);
    setEditActive(rule.active);
  };

  const handleSaveEdit = () => {
    if (!editing) return;
    updateRule.mutate({ id: editing.id, ratePercent: editRate, active: editActive });
    setEditing(null);
  };

  const handleCreate = () => {
    if (!newRegion.trim()) return;
    createRule.mutate({ region: newRegion.trim(), ratePercent: newRate, appliesTo: newAppliesTo.trim() || "All course sales" });
    setShowCreate(false);
    setNewRegion("");
    setNewRate(0);
    setNewAppliesTo("");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Percent size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Tax / VAT Settings</h1><HelpTooltip text="Region-based tax rules that are applied automatically at checkout based on the buyer's location." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Region-based tax rules applied at checkout.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New rule
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
        {isLoading ? (
          Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={3} />)
        ) : (
          rules?.map((rule) => (
            <div key={rule.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-line px-5 py-4 last:border-0 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{rule.region}</p>
                  <span
                    className={
                      rule.active
                        ? "rounded-full bg-success-tint px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-success"
                        : "rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft"
                    }
                  >
                    {rule.active ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{rule.appliesTo}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                <span className="font-mono text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">{rule.ratePercent}%</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => openEdit(rule)}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                >
                  <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(editing)}
        onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        title={`Edit tax rule - ${editing?.region ?? ""}`}
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSaveEdit} disabled={updateRule.isPending}>
              Save changes
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input
            label="Rate (%)"
            type="number"
            min={0}
            max={100}
            value={editRate}
            onChange={(e) => setEditRate(Number(e.target.value))}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEditActive((v) => !v)}
            className="flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line-strong px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink hover:border-ink"
          >
            {editActive ? <Check size={14} strokeWidth={2} className="text-success" /> : <X size={14} strokeWidth={2} className="text-ink-soft" />}
            {editActive ? "Active" : "Inactive"} - click to toggle
          </button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={showCreate}
        onClose={() => setShowCreate(false)}
        title="New tax rule"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowCreate(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={!newRegion.trim() || createRule.isPending}>
              Create rule
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Region" placeholder="e.g. Canada" value={newRegion} onChange={(e) => setNewRegion(e.target.value)} />
          <Input label="Rate (%)" type="number" min={0} max={100} value={newRate} onChange={(e) => setNewRate(Number(e.target.value))} />
          <Input label="Applies to" placeholder="e.g. Digital goods (GST)" value={newAppliesTo} onChange={(e) => setNewAppliesTo(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\finance\transactions\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Search, Receipt, Download } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useAdminTransactions, type AdminTransaction } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { DataTable, StatusPill, Select, Button , RefreshButton , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Completed", value: "completed" },
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Failed", value: "failed" },
  { label: "Refunded", value: "refunded" }
];

const GATEWAY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Stripe", value: "stripe" },
  { label: "PayPal", value: "paypal" },
  { label: "Easypaisa", value: "easypaisa" },
  { label: "JazzCash", value: "jazzcash" },
  { label: "Bank Transfer", value: "bank_transfer" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminTransaction["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info"> = {
  completed: "success",
  pending: "warning",
  failed: "error",
  refunded: "info"
};

export default function TransactionsLedgerPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [gateway, setGateway] = useState("");

  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminTransactions({
    search: search || undefined,
    status: status || undefined,
    gateway: gateway || undefined
  });

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<AdminTransaction, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Transaction",
        accessorKey: "id",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div>
            <div className="font-mono text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.id}</div>
            <div className="max-w-[220px] truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.courseTitle}</div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Learner",
        accessorKey: "learnerName",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Instructor",
        accessorKey: "instructorName",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Amount",
        accessorKey: "amount",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">
            {formatCurrency(row.original.amount, row.original.currency)}
          </span>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Commission",
        accessorKey: "commission",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <span className="font-mono text-ink-soft">
            {formatCurrency(row.original.commission, row.original.currency)}
          </span>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Gateway",
        accessorKey: "gateway",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="capitalize text-ink-soft">{(getValue() as string).replace("_", " ")}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as AdminTransaction["status"];
          return <StatusPill label={value} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Date",
        accessorKey: "createdAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      }
    ],
    []
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1240px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Receipt size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Transactions Ledger</h1><HelpTooltip text="A complete record of every payment processed through the platform, including the commission taken and which gateway was used." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Full financial transaction history across all payment gateways.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
          <Download size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Export CSV
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="mb-3 flex justify-end"><RefreshButton /></div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        emptyTitle="No transactions found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by ID, course, learner, or instructor"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All gateways" options={GATEWAY_OPTIONS} value={gateway} onChange={(e) => setGateway(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Search, History } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { DataTable, Select , RefreshButton , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

type AuditEntry = {
  id: string;
  actorName: string;
  actorRole: string;
  action: string;
  target: string;
  module: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

const MODULE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Users", value: "users" },
  { label: "Courses", value: "courses" },
  { label: "Finance", value: "finance" },
  { label: "Marketing", value: "marketing" },
  { label: "Settings", value: "settings" }
];

const AUDIT_LOG: AuditEntry[] = [
  { id: "log_1", actorName: "Abdul Rehman Irfan", actorRole: "Super Admin", action: "Approved payout batch", target: "PAYOUT-2026-0912", module: "finance", createdAt: "2026-08-06T14:20:00Z" },
  { id: "log_2", actorName: "Support Desk", actorRole: "Support Officer", action: "Resolved ticket", target: "TCK-4471", module: "users", createdAt: "2026-08-06T11:05:00Z" },
  { id: "log_3", actorName: "Finance Desk", actorRole: "Finance Officer", action: "Approved refund", target: "TXN-88213", module: "finance", createdAt: "2026-08-05T18:42:00Z" },
  { id: "log_4", actorName: "Abdul Rehman Irfan", actorRole: "Super Admin", action: "Updated commission tier", target: "Verified Instructor -> 25%", module: "settings", createdAt: "2026-08-05T09:15:00Z" },
  { id: "log_5", actorName: "Abdul Rehman Irfan", actorRole: "Super Admin", action: "Published homepage banner", target: "Founding Instructor Spots Open", module: "marketing", createdAt: "2026-08-04T16:30:00Z" },
  { id: "log_6", actorName: "Support Desk", actorRole: "Support Officer", action: "Suspended user account", target: "user_9910", module: "users", createdAt: "2026-08-03T10:02:00Z" },
  { id: "log_7", actorName: "Abdul Rehman Irfan", actorRole: "Super Admin", action: "Approved course for publish", target: "AI Automation with n8n", module: "courses", createdAt: "2026-08-02T13:48:00Z" }
];

export default function AuditLogViewerPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [moduleFilter, setModuleFilter] = useState("");

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return AUDIT_LOG.filter((entry) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        !search ||
        entry.actorName.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        entry.action.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
        entry.target.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
      const matchesModule = !moduleFilter || entry.module === moduleFilter;
      return matchesSearch && matchesModule;
    });
  }, [search, moduleFilter]);

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<AuditEntry, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Actor",
        accessorKey: "actorName",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div>
            <div className="font-medium text-ink">{row.original.actorName}</div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.actorRole}</div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      { header: "Action", accessorKey: "action", cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink">{getValue()}</span> },
      { header: "Target", accessorKey: "target", cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="font-mono text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span> },
      { header: "Module", accessorKey: "module", cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="capitalize text-ink-soft">{getValue() as string}</span> },
      { header: "Timestamp", accessorKey: "createdAt", cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span> }
    ],
    []
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <History size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Audit Log</h1><HelpTooltip text="A searchable, system-wide record of every action taken by internal staff, for accountability and compliance." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Searchable system-wide record of internal staff actions.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mb-3 flex justify-end"><RefreshButton /></div>

      <DataTable
        columns={columns}
        data={filtered}
        isLoading={false}
        emptyTitle="No log entries found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by actor, action, or target"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All modules" options={MODULE_OPTIONS} value={moduleFilter} onChange={(e) => setModuleFilter(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}
