FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\marketing\banners\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Image as ImageIcon, Plus, Pencil, Trash2, ArrowUp, ArrowDown } from "lucide-react";
import {
  useAdminBanners,
  useCreateBanner,
  useUpdateBanner,
  useDeleteBanner,
  useReorderBanner,
  type AdminBanner
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Textarea, Select, Modal, StatusPill, ShimmerKpiCard, EmptyState , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Draft", value: "draft" },
  { label: "Scheduled", value: "scheduled" },
  { label: "Active", value: "active" },
  { label: "Expired", value: "expired" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminBanner["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info" | "neutral"> = {
  active: "success",
  scheduled: "info",
  expired: "error",
  draft: "neutral"
};

type FormState = {
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  linkUrl: string;
  status: AdminBanner["status"];
  startsAt: string;
  endsAt: string;
};

const EMPTY_FORM: FormState = {
  title: "",
  subtitle: "",
  imageUrl: "",
  linkUrl: "",
  status: "draft",
  startsAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  endsAt: new Date(Date.now() + 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
};

export default function HomepageBannersPage() {
  const { data: banners, isLoading } = useAdminBanners();
  const createBanner = useCreateBanner();
  const updateBanner = useUpdateBanner();
  const deleteBanner = useDeleteBanner();
  const reorderBanner = useReorderBanner();

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AdminBanner | null>(null);
  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<AdminBanner | null>(null);

  const openCreate = () => {
    setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
    setShowCreate(true);
  };

  const openEdit = (banner: AdminBanner) => {
    setEditing(banner);
    setForm({
      title: banner.title,
      subtitle: banner.subtitle ?? "",
      imageUrl: banner.imageUrl,
      linkUrl: banner.linkUrl,
      status: banner.status,
      startsAt: banner.startsAt.slice(0, 10),
      endsAt: banner.endsAt.slice(0, 10)
    });
  };

  const closeModals = () => {
    setShowCreate(false);
    setEditing(null);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!form.title.trim()) return;
    const payload = {
      title: form.title.trim(),
      subtitle: form.subtitle.trim() || undefined,
      imageUrl: form.imageUrl.trim() || `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(form.title)}/1200/400`,
      linkUrl: form.linkUrl.trim() || "/",
      status: form.status,
      startsAt: new Date(form.startsAt).toISOString(),
      endsAt: new Date(form.endsAt).toISOString()
    };
    if (editing) {
      updateBanner.mutate({ id: editing.id, ...payload });
    } else {
      createBanner.mutate(payload);
    }
    closeModals();
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    deleteBanner.mutate(deleteTarget.id);
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  };

  const modalOpen = showCreate || Boolean(editing);

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <ImageIcon size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Homepage Banners</h1><HelpTooltip text="Promotional banners shown on the homepage. Use the arrows to reorder, and schedule start/end dates to control when each one is active." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Create, schedule, and reorder homepage promotional banners.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={openCreate}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New banner
        </Button>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !banners || banners.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<ImageIcon size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No banners yet" description="Create your first homepage banner." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {banners.map((banner, index) => (
            <Card key={banner.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <div className="h-[64px] w-[140px] shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim">
                {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                <img src={banner.imageUrl} alt={banner.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{banner.title}</p>
                  <StatusPill label={banner.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[banner.status]} />
                </div>
                {banner.subtitle && <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{banner.subtitle}</p>}
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft/70">
                  {formatDate(banner.startsAt)} - {formatDate(banner.endsAt)} - links to {banner.linkUrl}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={index === 0}
                  onClick={() => reorderBanner.mutate({ id: banner.id, direction: "up" })}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink disabled:opacity-30"
                >
                  <ArrowUp size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={index === banners.length - 1}
                  onClick={() => reorderBanner.mutate({ id: banner.id, direction: "down" })}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink disabled:opacity-30"
                >
                  <ArrowDown size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => openEdit(banner)}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                >
                  <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(banner)}
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={modalOpen}
        onClose={closeModals}
        title={editing ? "Edit banner" : "New banner"}
        size="lg"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={closeModals}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSave} disabled={!form.title.trim() || createBanner.isPending || updateBanner.isPending}>
              {editing ? "Save changes" : "Create banner"}
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Title" value={form.title} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, title: e.target.value })} placeholder="e.g. Founding Instructor Spots Open" />
          <Textarea label="Subtitle" value={form.subtitle} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, subtitle: e.target.value })} rows={2} placeholder="Optional supporting line" />
          <Input label="Image URL" value={form.imageUrl} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, imageUrl: e.target.value })} placeholder="Leave blank to use a placeholder image" />
          <Input label="Link URL" value={form.linkUrl} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, linkUrl: e.target.value })} placeholder="/courses" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <Select label="Status" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={form.status} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, status: e.target.value as AdminBanner["status"] })} />
            <Input label="Starts" type="date" value={form.startsAt} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, startsAt: e.target.value })} />
            <Input label="Ends" type="date" value={form.endsAt} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, endsAt: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(deleteTarget)}
        onClose={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
        title="Delete banner?"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleDelete} disabled={deleteBanner.isPending}>
              Delete
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          This will remove <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{deleteTarget?.title}</span> from the homepage rotation.
        </p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\marketing\coupons\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Ticket, Plus, Trash2, Search } from "lucide-react";
import {
  useAdminCoupons,
  useCreateCoupon,
  useToggleCouponActive,
  useDeleteCoupon,
  type AdminCoupon
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Card, Input, Select, Modal, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Percent off", value: "percent" },
  { label: "Fixed amount off", value: "fixed" }
];

const SCOPE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Platform-wide", value: "platform_wide" },
  { label: "Category", value: "category" },
  { label: "Course", value: "course" }
];

export default function CouponManagementPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const { data: coupons, isLoading } = useAdminCoupons({ search: search || undefined });
  const createCoupon = useCreateCoupon();
  const toggleActive = useToggleCouponActive();
  const deleteCoupon = useDeleteCoupon();

  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [type, setType] = useState<AdminCoupon["type"]>("percent");
  const [value, setValue] = useState(10);
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<AdminCoupon["scope"]>("platform_wide");
  const [usageLimit, setUsageLimit] = useState(100);
  const [expiresAt, setExpiresAt] = useState(new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<AdminCoupon | null>(null);

  const resetForm = () => {
    setCode("");
    setType("percent");
    setValue(10);
    setScope("platform_wide");
    setUsageLimit(100);
    setExpiresAt(new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  };

  const handleCreate = () => {
    if (!code.trim()) return;
    createCoupon.mutate({
      code: code.trim().toUpperCase(),
      type,
      value,
      scope,
      usageLimit,
      expiresAt: new Date(expiresAt).toISOString(),
      active: true
    });
    setShowCreate(false);
    resetForm();
  };

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    deleteCoupon.mutate(deleteTarget.id);
    setDeleteTarget(null);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Ticket size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Coupon Management</h1><HelpTooltip text="Platform-wide or scoped discount codes learners can apply at checkout. Track usage against each coupon's limit here." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Platform-wide and scoped promotional codes.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New coupon
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="relative mb-5 max-w-[320px]">
        <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
        <input
          placeholder="Search coupon code"
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
        />
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={5} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !coupons || coupons.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Ticket size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No coupons found" description="Try a different search, or create a new coupon." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {coupons.map((coupon) => {
            const usagePct = coupon.usageLimit > 0 ? Math.round((coupon.usageCount / coupon.usageLimit) * 100) : 0;
            return (
              <Card key={coupon.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <span className="rounded-control border border-line-strong px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">
                      {coupon.code}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={
                        coupon.active
                          ? "rounded-full bg-success-tint px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-success"
                          : "rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft"
                      }
                    >
                      {coupon.active ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                    {coupon.type === "percent" ? `${coupon.value}% off` : `$${coupon.value} off`} - {coupon.scope.replace("_", " ")} -
                    {" "}{coupon.usageCount}/{coupon.usageLimit} used ({usagePct}%) - expires {formatDate(coupon.expiresAt)}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => toggleActive.mutate(coupon.id)}>
                    {coupon.active ? "Deactivate" : "Activate"}
                  </Button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(coupon)}
                    className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={showCreate}
        onClose={() => setShowCreate(false)}
        title="New coupon"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowCreate(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={!code.trim() || createCoupon.isPending}>
              Create coupon
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Coupon code" value={code} onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())} placeholder="e.g. SUMMER20" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <Select label="Discount type" options={TYPE_OPTIONS} value={type} onChange={(e) => setType(e.target.value as AdminCoupon["type"])} />
            <Input
              label={type === "percent" ? "Percent off" : "Amount off ($)"}
              type="number"
              min={0}
              value={value}
              onChange={(e) => setValue(Number(e.target.value))}
            />
          </div>
          <Select label="Scope" options={SCOPE_OPTIONS} value={scope} onChange={(e) => setScope(e.target.value as AdminCoupon["scope"])} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <Input label="Usage limit" type="number" min={1} value={usageLimit} onChange={(e) => setUsageLimit(Number(e.target.value))} />
            <Input label="Expires" type="date" value={expiresAt} onChange={(e) => setExpiresAt(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(deleteTarget)}
        onClose={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
        title="Delete coupon?"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleDelete} disabled={deleteCoupon.isPending}>
              Delete
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p className="text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
          This will permanently remove <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{deleteTarget?.code}</span>. It can no
          longer be applied at checkout.
        </p>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Megaphone, Crown } from "lucide-react";
import { useAdminPromotionSales, type PromotionSale } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Card, StatusPill, ShimmerKpiCard, ShimmerTableRow, EmptyState , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const TIER_TONE: Record<PromotionSale["tier"], "warning" | "info" | "success"> = {
  bronze: "warning",
  silver: "info",
  gold: "success"
};

export default function PromotionSalesPage() {
  const { data: sales, isLoading } = useAdminPromotionSales();

  const now = Date.now();
  const active = (sales ?? []).filter((s) => new Date(s.expiresAt).getTime() > now);
  const totalRevenue = (sales ?? []).reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.amount, 0);
  const tierCounts = {
    gold: (sales ?? []).filter((s) => s.tier === "gold").length,
    silver: (sales ?? []).filter((s) => s.tier === "silver").length,
    bronze: (sales ?? []).filter((s) => s.tier === "bronze").length
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1100px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <Megaphone size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Promotion Package Sales</h1><HelpTooltip text="Revenue and activity from instructors purchasing Bronze/Silver/Gold visibility boosts for their courses." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Instructor purchases of Bronze/Silver/Gold visibility boosts.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Total revenue</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{formatCurrency(totalRevenue, "USD")}</p>
          </Card>
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Currently active</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{active.length}</p>
          </Card>
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
              <Crown size={12} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Gold sold
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{tierCounts.gold}</p>
          </Card>
          <Card className="p-5">
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">Silver + Bronze sold</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{tierCounts.silver + tierCounts.bronze}</p>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !sales || sales.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<Megaphone size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="No promotion sales yet" description="Boost package purchases will show up here." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {sales.map((sale) => {
            const isActive = new Date(sale.expiresAt).getTime() > now;
            return (
              <Card key={sale.id} className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <p className="truncate text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{sale.courseTitle}</p>
                    <StatusPill label={sale.tier} tone={TIER_TONE[sale.tier]} />
                    {!isActive && <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-ink-soft">Expired</span>}
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                    {sale.instructorName} - purchased {formatDate(sale.purchasedAt)} - {isActive ? "expires" : "expired"} {formatDate(sale.expiresAt)}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {formatCurrency(sale.amount, sale.currency)}
                </span>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ShieldCheck, FileWarning, CheckCircle2, Trash2, ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Select, StatusPill, EmptyState, HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatRelativeTime } from "@saachi/utils";

type ContentType = "course_description" | "instructor_bio" | "banner_copy" | "certificate_note";
type FlagStatus = "pending" | "removed" | "dismissed" | "warned";

type FlaggedContent = {
  id: string;
  type: ContentType;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  author: string;
  flaggedBy: string;
  flagReason: string;
  status: FlagStatus;
  createdAt: string;
};

const TYPE_LABEL: Record<ContentType, string> = {
  course_description: "Course description",
  instructor_bio: "Instructor bio",
  banner_copy: "Banner copy",
  certificate_note: "Certificate note"
};

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Removed", value: "removed" },
  { label: "Dismissed", value: "dismissed" },
  { label: "Warned", value: "warned" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<FlagStatus, "warning" | "error" | "neutral" | "info"> = {
  pending: "warning",
  removed: "error",
  dismissed: "neutral",
  warned: "info"
};

const INITIAL_CONTENT: FlaggedContent[] = [
  {
    id: "c1",
    type: "course_description",
    title: "AI Automation with n8n",
    body: "This course guarantees you will make $10,000 in your first month using these automation secrets nobody talks about.",
    author: "Zeeshan Tariq",
    flaggedBy: "Automated filter",
    flagReason: "Unverifiable earnings claim",
    status: "pending",
    createdAt: "2026-08-08T08:20:00Z"
  },
  {
    id: "c2",
    type: "instructor_bio",
    title: "Bilal Ahmed - Instructor Bio",
    body: "Ex-Google, ex-Meta, ex-OpenAI senior engineer with 15 years building production AI systems at scale.",
    author: "Bilal Ahmed",
    flaggedBy: "Fatima Noor",
    flagReason: "Unverified employment claims",
    status: "pending",
    createdAt: "2026-08-07T15:10:00Z"
  }
];

export default function ContentModerationPage() {
  const [content, setContent] = useState(INITIAL_CONTENT);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("pending");

  const filtered = content.filter((c) => !statusFilter || c.status === statusFilter);

  const resolve = (id: string, status: FlagStatus) => {
    setContent((prev) => prev.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, status } : c)));
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <ShieldCheck size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Content Moderation</h1>
              <HelpTooltip text="Flagged platform content outside of reviews and forum posts - course descriptions, instructor bios, banner copy, and certificate notes." />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Miscellaneous platform content flagged for policy review.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="w-[180px]">
          <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<FileWarning size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="Nothing flagged" description="No content matches this filter." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {filtered.map((c) => (
            <Card key={c.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5">
              <div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{c.title}</p>
                  <span className="rounded-full border border-line-strong px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-ink-soft">
                    {TYPE_LABEL[c.type]}
                  </span>
                  <StatusPill label={c.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[c.status]} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                  by {c.author} - {formatRelativeTime(c.createdAt)}
                </p>
              </div>

              <p className="rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim p-3.5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink">
                {c.body}
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                <ShieldAlert size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-error" />
                Flagged by {c.flaggedBy} - reason: {c.flagReason}
              </div>

              {c.status === "pending" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve(c.id, "dismissed")}>
                    <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Dismiss flag
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve(c.id, "warned")}>
                    Warn author
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={() => resolve(c.id, "removed")}>
                    <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Remove content
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { MessagesSquare, Trash2, ShieldAlert, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";

  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Removed", value: "removed" },
  { label: "Dismissed", value: "dismissed" },

  removed: "error",
  dismissed: "neutral",
  warned: "info"
};

          </div>

        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !posts || posts.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<MessagesSquare size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="Nothing flagged" description="No forum posts match this filter." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {posts.map((post) => (

              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{post.authorName}</p>
                  <StatusPill label={post.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[post.status]} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                  in {post.courseTitle} - {formatRelativeTime(post.createdAt)}
                </p>
              </div>

              <p className="rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim p-3.5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink">
                {post.body}
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                <ShieldAlert size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-error" />
                Flagged by {post.flaggedBy} - reason: {post.flagReason}
              </div>

              {post.status === "pending" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: post.id, status: "dismissed" })}>
                    <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Dismiss flag
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: post.id, status: "warned" })}>
                    Warn user
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: post.id, status: "removed" })}>
                    <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Remove post
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" },
  { label: "Removed", value: "removed" },
  { label: "Dismissed", value: "dismissed" },
  { label: "Warned", value: "warned" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<FlaggedReview["status"], "warning" | "error" | "neutral" | "info"> = {
  pending: "warning",
  removed: "error",
  dismissed: "neutral",
  warned: "info"
};

export default function FlaggedReviewsPage() {
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("pending");
  const { data: reviews, isLoading } = useFlaggedReviews({ status: statusFilter || undefined });
  const resolve = useResolveFlaggedReview();

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">

            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Course reviews reported by instructors, learners, or automated filters.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="w-[180px]">
          <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={4} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : !reviews || reviews.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState icon={<MessageSquareWarning size={22} strokeWidth={1.75} />} title="Nothing flagged" description="No reviews match this filter." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {reviews.map((review) => (
            <Card key={review.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{review.authorName}</p>
                    <StatusPill label={review.status} tone={STATUS_TONE[review.status]} />
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                    on {review.courseTitle} - {"\u2605".repeat(review.rating)}{"\u2606".repeat(5 - review.rating)} - {formatRelativeTime(review.createdAt)}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <p className="rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim p-3.5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink">
                {review.comment}
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                <ShieldAlert size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-error" />
                Flagged by {review.flaggedBy} - reason: {review.flagReason}
              </div>

              {review.status === "pending" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: review.id, status: "dismissed" })}>
                    <CheckCircle2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Dismiss flag
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: review.id, status: "warned" })}>
                    Warn user
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={() => resolve.mutate({ id: review.id, status: "removed" })}>
                    <Trash2 size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Remove review
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\settings\commission-tiers\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Landmark, Save } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, ShimmerKpiCard , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";

type Tier = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  criteria: string;
  ratePercent: number;
};

const INITIAL_TIERS: Tier[] = [
  { id: "new", name: "New Instructor", criteria: "Just approved, 0-2 published courses", ratePercent: 30 },
  { id: "verified", name: "Verified Instructor", criteria: "Identity verified, 3+ courses, 4.0+ rating", ratePercent: 25 },
  { id: "top_rated", name: "Top-Rated Instructor", criteria: "10+ courses, 4.5+ rating, consistent sales", ratePercent: 20 },
  { id: "partner", name: "Partner Instructor", criteria: "Invited/negotiated status", ratePercent: 15 }
];

export default function CommissionTierConfigurationPage() {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [tiers, setTiers] = useState(INITIAL_TIERS);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIsLoading(false), 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  const updateRate = (id: string, value: number) => {
    setTiers((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, ratePercent: value } : t)));
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    setSaving(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSaving(false);
      toast.success("Commission tiers updated");
    }, 700);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <Landmark size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Commission Tier Configuration</h1><HelpTooltip text="The commission percentage the platform retains from each course sale, based on the instructor's trust tier." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Trust-tier commission rates applied to instructor course sales.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving || isLoading}>
          <Save size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save changes"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerKpiCard key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {tiers.map((tier) => (
            <Card key={tier.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{tier.name}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{tier.criteria}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full sm:w-[140px]">
                <Input
                  label="Commission %"
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  max={100}
                  value={tier.ratePercent}
                  onChange={(e) => updateRate(tier.id, Number(e.target.value))}
                />
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\settings\legal-documents\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { ScrollText, Pencil, History } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Textarea, Modal, ShimmerTableRow , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

type LegalDoc = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  version: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  content: string;
};

const INITIAL_DOCS: LegalDoc[] = [
  { id: "terms", title: "Terms & Conditions", version: "v1.0", updatedAt: "2026-06-01T00:00:00Z", content: "Draft pending client legal counsel review." },
  { id: "privacy", title: "Privacy Policy", version: "v1.0", updatedAt: "2026-06-01T00:00:00Z", content: "Draft pending client legal counsel review." },
  { id: "refund", title: "Refund Policy", version: "v1.0", updatedAt: "2026-06-01T00:00:00Z", content: "7-day / 30% completion threshold refund window - to be finalized." },
  { id: "instructor_agreement", title: "Instructor Agreement", version: "v1.0", updatedAt: "2026-06-01T00:00:00Z", content: "Draft pending client legal counsel review." }
];

export default function LegalDocumentManagementPage() {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [docs, setDocs] = useState(INITIAL_DOCS);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<LegalDoc | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIsLoading(false), 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  const openEdit = (doc: LegalDoc) => {
    setEditing(doc);
    setDraft(doc.content);
  };

  const handlePublish = () => {
    if (!editing) return;
    const [major, minor] = editing.version.replace("v", "").split(".").map(Number);
    const nextVersion = `v${major}.${minor + 1}`;
    setDocs((prev) =>
      prev.map((d) =>
        d.id === editing.id ? { ...d, content: draft, version: nextVersion, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } : d
      )
    );
    toast.success(`Published ${editing.title} ${nextVersion}`);
    setEditing(null);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <ScrollText size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Legal Document Management</h1><HelpTooltip text="Versioned legal documents shown to users. Publishing an edit bumps the version number and updates the live copy." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Versioned editor for Terms, Privacy, Refund Policy, and Instructor Agreement.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={2} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {docs.map((doc) => (
            <Card key={doc.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{doc.title}</p>
                  <span className="rounded-full border border-line-strong px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-soft">{doc.version}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
                  <History size={12} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Last updated {formatDate(doc.updatedAt)}
                </p>
              </div>
              <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => openEdit(doc)}>
                <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Edit
              </Button>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(editing)}
        onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        title={`Edit - ${editing?.title ?? ""}`}
        size="lg"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</Button>
            <Button onClick={handlePublish}>Publish new version</Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <Textarea label="Document content" rows={12} value={draft} onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)} />
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\settings\payment-gateways\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CreditCard, Pencil, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, Modal , HelpTooltip , Switch } from "@saachi/ui";

type Gateway = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  region: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  apiKeyMasked: string;
};

const INITIAL_GATEWAYS: Gateway[] = [
  { id: "stripe", name: "Stripe", region: "International", enabled: true, apiKeyMasked: "sk_live_********4f21" },
  { id: "paypal", name: "PayPal", region: "International", enabled: true, apiKeyMasked: "client_********a9e0" },
  { id: "easypaisa", name: "Easypaisa", region: "Pakistan", enabled: false, apiKeyMasked: "not configured" },
  { id: "jazzcash", name: "JazzCash", region: "Pakistan", enabled: false, apiKeyMasked: "not configured" },
  { id: "bank_transfer", name: "Bank Transfer", region: "Pakistan", enabled: true, apiKeyMasked: "manual - no key" }
];

export default function PaymentGatewayConfigurationPage() {
  const [gateways, setGateways] = useState(INITIAL_GATEWAYS);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Gateway | null>(null);
  const [newKey, setNewKey] = useState("");
  const [showKey, setShowKey] = useState(false);

  const toggleEnabled = (id: string) => {
    setGateways((prev) => prev.map((g) => (g.id === id ? { ...g, enabled: !g.enabled } : g)));
  };

  const openEdit = (gateway: Gateway) => {
    setEditing(gateway);
    setNewKey("");
    setShowKey(false);
  };

  const handleSaveKey = () => {
    if (!editing || !newKey.trim()) return;
    const masked = `********${newKey.trim().slice(-4)}`;
    setGateways((prev) => prev.map((g) => (g.id === editing.id ? { ...g, apiKeyMasked: masked } : g)));
    setEditing(null);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[900px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <CreditCard size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Payment Gateway Configuration</h1><HelpTooltip text="Enable or disable payment gateways and manage their API keys. Keys are masked once saved." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Enable gateways and manage API keys (masked at rest).</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {gateways.map((gateway) => (
          <Card key={gateway.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{gateway.name}</p>
                <span className="rounded-full border border-line-strong px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-ink-soft">{gateway.region}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{gateway.apiKeyMasked}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
              <Switch checked={gateway.enabled} onChange={() => toggleEnabled(gateway.id)} />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => openEdit(gateway)}
                className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
              >
                <Pencil size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Modal
        open={Boolean(editing)}
        onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        title={`Update API key - ${editing?.name ?? ""}`}
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSaveKey} disabled={!newKey.trim()}>Save key</Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="relative">
          <Input
            label="New API key / secret"
            type={showKey ? "text" : "password"}
            value={newKey}
            onChange={(e) => setNewKey(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Paste the new secret key"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowKey((v) => !v)}
            className="absolute right-3 top-[34px] text-ink-soft hover:text-ink"
          >
            {showKey ? <EyeOff size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <Eye size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { ShieldCheck, Plus, Check, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, Select, Modal, Badge, ShimmerTableRow , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";

type StaffRole = "super_admin" | "admin" | "finance_officer" | "support_officer";

type StaffAccount = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: StaffRole;
};

const ROLE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Super Admin", value: "super_admin" },
  { label: "Admin", value: "admin" },
  { label: "Finance Officer", value: "finance_officer" },
  { label: "Support Officer", value: "support_officer" }
];

const ROLE_LABEL: Record<StaffRole, string> = {
  super_admin: "Super Admin",
  admin: "Admin",
  finance_officer: "Finance Officer",
  support_officer: "Support Officer"
};

const PERMISSION_MATRIX: { capability: string; roles: Record<StaffRole, boolean> }[] = [
  { capability: "Manage internal admin accounts", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: false, finance_officer: false, support_officer: false } },
  { capability: "Approve/reject courses", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: true, finance_officer: false, support_officer: false } },
  { capability: "Process payouts", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: true, finance_officer: true, support_officer: false } },
  { capability: "Issue refunds", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: true, finance_officer: true, support_officer: false } },
  { capability: "Ban/suspend users", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: true, finance_officer: false, support_officer: true } },
  { capability: "Handle support tickets", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: true, finance_officer: false, support_officer: true } },
  { capability: "Configure commission rates", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: false, finance_officer: false, support_officer: false } },
  { capability: "View financial reports", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: true, finance_officer: true, support_officer: false } },
  { capability: "Configure payment gateways", roles: { super_admin: true, admin: false, finance_officer: false, support_officer: false } }
];

const INITIAL_STAFF: StaffAccount[] = [
  { id: "1", name: "Abdul Rehman Irfan", email: "abdulrehman@saachi.ai", role: "super_admin" },
  { id: "2", name: "Finance Desk", email: "finance@saachi.ai", role: "finance_officer" },
  { id: "3", name: "Support Desk", email: "support@saachi.ai", role: "support_officer" }
];

export default function RolesPermissionsPage() {
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [staff, setStaff] = useState(INITIAL_STAFF);
  const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState<StaffRole>("support_officer");

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setIsLoading(false), 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  const handleCreate = () => {
    if (!name.trim() || !email.trim()) return;
    setStaff((prev) => [...prev, { id: String(Date.now()), name: name.trim(), email: email.trim(), role }]);
    toast.success(`Added ${name.trim()} as ${ROLE_LABEL[role]}`);
    setShowCreate(false);
    setName("");
    setEmail("");
    setRole("support_officer");
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1000px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <ShieldCheck size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Roles & Permissions</h1><HelpTooltip text="Internal staff accounts and what each role is permitted to do, shown in the permission matrix below." /></div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Internal staff accounts and their permission scope.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)} disabled={isLoading}>
          <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> New staff account
        </Button>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="mb-8 overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={2} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col gap-3">
          {staff.map((account) => (
            <Card key={account.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{account.name}</p>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{account.email}</p>
              </div>
              <Badge>{ROLE_LABEL[account.role]}</Badge>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="mb-3 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Permission matrix</p>
      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
            <ShimmerTableRow key={i} columns={5} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[720px] border-collapse text-[13px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-line text-left text-ink-soft">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Capability</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Super Admin</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Admin</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Finance Officer</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">Support Officer</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {PERMISSION_MATRIX.map((row) => (
                <tr key={row.capability} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-ink">{row.capability}</td>
                  {(["super_admin", "admin", "finance_officer", "support_officer"] as StaffRole[]).map((r) => (
                    <td key={r} className="px-4 py-3">
                      {row.roles[r] ? (
                        <Check size={15} strokeWidth={2} className="text-success" />
                      ) : (
                        <X size={15} strokeWidth={2} className="text-ink-soft/40" />
                      )}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      <Modal
        open={showCreate}
        onClose={() => setShowCreate(false)}
        title="New staff account"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowCreate(false)}>Cancel</Button>
            <Button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={!name.trim() || !email.trim()}>Create account</Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Full name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          <Input label="Email" type="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
          <Select label="Role" options={ROLE_OPTIONS} value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value as StaffRole)} />
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search, LifeBuoy, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useAdminTickets, type SupportTicket } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { DataTable, StatusPill, Select , RefreshButton , HelpTooltip } from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatRelativeTime } from "@saachi/utils";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Open", value: "open" },
  { label: "In progress", value: "in_progress" },
  { label: "Resolved", value: "resolved" },
  { label: "Closed", value: "closed" }
];

const PRIORITY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Low", value: "low" },
  { label: "Medium", value: "medium" },
  { label: "High", value: "high" },
  { label: "Urgent", value: "urgent" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<SupportTicket["status"], "warning" | "info" | "success" | "neutral"> = {
  open: "warning",
  in_progress: "info",
  resolved: "success",
  closed: "neutral"
};

const PRIORITY_TONE: Record<SupportTicket["priority"], "neutral" | "info" | "warning" | "error"> = {
  low: "neutral",
  medium: "info",
  high: "warning",
  urgent: "error"
};

export default function SupportTicketQueuePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState("");

  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminTickets({
    search: search || undefined,
    status: status || undefined,
    priority: priority || undefined
  });

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<SupportTicket, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        header: "Subject",
        accessorKey: "subject",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="max-w-[280px]">
            <div className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{row.original.subject}</div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.requesterName} - {row.original.category}</div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Priority",
        accessorKey: "priority",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as SupportTicket["priority"];
          return <StatusPill label={value} tone={PRIORITY_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => {
          const value = getValue() as SupportTicket["status"];
          return <StatusPill label={value.replace("_", " ")} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />;
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Assigned to",
        accessorKey: "assignedTo",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{(getValue() as string) ?? "Unassigned"}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "SLA",
        accessorKey: "slaDueAt",
        cell: ({ row }) => {
          const overdue = new Date(row.original.slaDueAt).getTime() < Date.now() && row.original.status !== "resolved" && row.original.status !== "closed";
          return overdue ? (
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium text-error">
              <AlertTriangle size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Overdue
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatRelativeTime(row.original.slaDueAt)}</span>
          );
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Created",
        accessorKey: "createdAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatRelativeTime(getValue() as string)}</span>
      }
    ],
    []
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
          <LifeBuoy size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Support Ticket Queue</h1><HelpTooltip text="Learner and instructor support requests tracked against their SLA due time. Overdue tickets are highlighted in red." /></div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Learner and instructor support requests, tracked against SLA.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="admin-clickable-table"><DataTable 
        columns={columns}
        data={data ?? []}
        isLoading={isLoading}
        emptyTitle="No tickets found"
        emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
        onRowClick={(row) => router.push(`/support/tickets/${row.id}`)}
        toolbar={
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
              <input
                placeholder="Search by subject or requester"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
              <Select placeholder="All priorities" options={PRIORITY_OPTIONS} value={priority} onChange={(e) => setPriority(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      /></div>    </main>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\admin\app\(console)\users\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import {
  Search,
  UserCog,
  Users,
  GraduationCap,
  Briefcase,
  Building2,
  Download,
  Eye,
  Ban,
  ShieldCheck,
  KeyRound,
  X
} from "lucide-react";
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useAdminUserList, type AdminUser } from "@saachi/api-client";
import {
  DataTable,
  StatusPill,
  Select,
  RefreshButton,
  HelpTooltip,
  Button,
  Card,
  RowActionsMenu,
  type RowAction
} from "@saachi/ui";
import { formatDate } from "@saachi/utils";

const ROLE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Learner", value: "learner" },
  { label: "Instructor", value: "instructor" },
  { label: "Corporate", value: "corporate" },
  { label: "Admin", value: "admin" }
];

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Active", value: "active" },
  { label: "Suspended", value: "suspended" },
  { label: "Banned", value: "banned" },
  { label: "Pending", value: "pending" }
];

const STATUS_TONE: Record<AdminUser["status"], "success" | "warning" | "error" | "info"> = {
  active: "success",
  suspended: "warning",
  banned: "error",
  pending: "info"
};

const STATUS_CYCLE: AdminUser["status"][] = ["active", "suspended", "banned", "pending"];

export default function AdminUsersPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [statusPopoverId, setStatusPopoverId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [localOverrides, setLocalOverrides] = useState<Record<string, AdminUser["status"]>>({});

  const { data, isLoading } = useAdminUserList({
    search: search || undefined,
    role: role || undefined,
    status: status || undefined
  });

  const rows = useMemo(() => {
    const base = data ?? [];
    return base.map((r) => (localOverrides[r.id] ? { ...r, status: localOverrides[r.id] } : r));
  }, [data, localOverrides]);

  const summary = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      total: rows.length,
      learners: rows.filter((r) => r.role === "learner").length,
      instructors: rows.filter((r) => r.role === "instructor").length,
      corporate: rows.filter((r) => r.role === "corporate").length
    };
  }, [rows]);

  const toggleSelected = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) {
        next.delete(id);
      } else {
        next.add(id);
      }
      return next;
    });
  };

  const toggleSelectAll = () => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      if (prev.size === rows.length && rows.length > 0) return new Set();
      return new Set(rows.map((r) => r.id));
    });
  };

  const clearSelection = () => setSelectedIds(new Set());

  const setUserStatus = (id: string, newStatus: AdminUser["status"]) => {
    setLocalOverrides((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: newStatus }));
    setStatusPopoverId(null);
    toast.success(`Status updated to ${newStatus}`);
  };

  const handleResetPassword = (userRow: AdminUser) => {
    toast.success(`Password reset email sent to ${userRow.email}`);
  };

  const handleBulkSuspend = () => {
    setLocalOverrides((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      selectedIds.forEach((id) => {
        next[id] = "suspended";
      });
      return next;
    });
    toast.success(`${selectedIds.size} user(s) suspended`);
    clearSelection();
  };

  const handleBulkExport = () => {
    const targetRows = rows.filter((r) => selectedIds.has(r.id));
    const header = ["Name", "Email", "Role", "Status", "Location", "Joined", "Last Active"];
    const csvRows = targetRows.map((r) => [
      r.fullName,
      r.email,
      r.role,
      r.status,
      r.location ?? "",
      formatDate(r.joinedAt),
      formatDate(r.lastActiveAt)
    ]);
    const csv = [header, ...csvRows].map((rowArr) => rowArr.map((v) => `"${String(v).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(",")).join("\n");
    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `saachi-users-selected-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleExportAll = () => {
    const targetRows = rows;
    const header = ["Name", "Email", "Role", "Status", "Location", "Joined", "Last Active"];
    const csvRows = targetRows.map((r) => [
      r.fullName,
      r.email,
      r.role,
      r.status,
      r.location ?? "",
      formatDate(r.joinedAt),
      formatDate(r.lastActiveAt)
    ]);
    const csv = [header, ...csvRows].map((rowArr) => rowArr.map((v) => `"${String(v).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(",")).join("\n");
    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `saachi-users-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const buildActions = (row: AdminUser): RowAction[] => [
    {
      id: "view",
      label: "View profile",
      icon: <Eye size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />,
      onClick: () => router.push(`/users/${row.id}`)
    },
    {
      id: "reset",
      label: "Reset password",
      icon: <KeyRound size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />,
      onClick: () => handleResetPassword(row)
    },
    {
      id: "suspend",
      label: row.status === "suspended" ? "Reinstate" : "Suspend",
      icon: <ShieldCheck size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />,
      onClick: () => setUserStatus(row.id, row.status === "suspended" ? "active" : "suspended")
    },
    {
      id: "ban",
      label: row.status === "banned" ? "Unban" : "Ban",
      icon: <Ban size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />,
      tone: "danger",
      onClick: () => setUserStatus(row.id, row.status === "banned" ? "active" : "banned")
    }
  ];

  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<AdminUser, any>[]>(
    () => [
      {
        id: "select",
        header: () => (
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={rows.length > 0 && selectedIds.size === rows.length}
            onChange={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              toggleSelectAll();
            }}
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="h-4 w-4 cursor-pointer rounded border-line-strong accent-[var(--color-ink)]"
          />
        ),
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={selectedIds.has(row.original.id)}
            onChange={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              toggleSelected(row.original.id);
            }}
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="h-4 w-4 cursor-pointer rounded border-line-strong accent-[var(--color-ink)]"
          />
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Name",
        accessorKey: "fullName",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-line bg-paper-dim text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">
              {row.original.avatarUrl ? (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={row.original.avatarUrl} alt={row.original.fullName} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
              ) : (
                row.original.fullName.charAt(0)
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="font-medium text-ink">{row.original.fullName}</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{row.original.email}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      },
      {
        header: "Role",
        accessorKey: "role",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="capitalize text-ink-soft">{getValue()}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Status",
        accessorKey: "status",
        cell: ({ row }) => {
          const value = row.original.status;
          const isOpen = statusPopoverId === row.original.id;
          return (
            <div className="relative">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  setStatusPopoverId(isOpen ? null : row.original.id);
                }}
                className="cursor-pointer"
              >
                <StatusPill label={value} tone={STATUS_TONE[value]} />
              </button>
              {isOpen && (
                <div
                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                  className="absolute left-0 top-[calc(100%+4px)] z-30 w-[160px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col p-1.5">
                    {STATUS_CYCLE.map((s) => (
                      <button
                        key={s}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setUserStatus(row.original.id, s)}
                        className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-control px-3 py-1.5 text-left text-[12.5px] capitalize transition-colors duration-100 ease-out ${
                          s === value ? "bg-paper-dim text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {s}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        }
      },
      {
        header: "Location",
        accessorKey: "location",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{getValue() ?? "-"}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Joined",
        accessorKey: "joinedAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      },
      {
        header: "Last active",
        accessorKey: "lastActiveAt",
        cell: ({ getValue }) => <span className="text-ink-soft">{formatDate(getValue() as string)}</span>
      },
      {
        id: "actions",
        header: "",
        cell: ({ row }) => (
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <RowActionsMenu actions={buildActions(row.original)} ariaLabel={`Actions for ${row.original.fullName}`} />
          </div>
        )
      }
    ],
    [rows, selectedIds, statusPopoverId]
  );

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px] px-4 py-8 md:px-8 md:py-10">
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-control border border-line text-ink">
            <UserCog size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <h1 className="text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">User Management</h1>
              <HelpTooltip text="All learners, instructors, and corporate accounts on the platform. Click a row to view full profile, activity, and moderation controls. Use checkboxes to select multiple users for bulk actions." />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Learners, instructors, and corporate accounts.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={handleExportAll}>
            <Download size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Export CSV
          </Button>
          <RefreshButton />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <Card className="p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            <Users size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Total users
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1.5 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{summary.total}</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            <GraduationCap size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Learners
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1.5 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{summary.learners}</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            <Briefcase size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Instructors
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1.5 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{summary.instructors}</p>
        </Card>
        <Card className="p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            <Building2 size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Corporate
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1.5 font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">{summary.corporate}</p>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {selectedIds.size > 0 && (
        <div className="sticky top-[70px] z-20 mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-control border border-ink bg-ink px-4 py-3 text-paper shadow-lg">
          <span className="text-[13px] font-medium">{selectedIds.size} user(s) selected</span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={handleBulkExport}>
              <Download size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Export selected
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={handleBulkSuspend}>
              <ShieldCheck size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Bulk suspend
            </Button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={clearSelection}
              className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-paper/80 transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper/10 hover:text-paper"
              aria-label="Clear selection"
            >
              <X size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="admin-clickable-table">
        <DataTable
          columns={columns}
          data={rows}
          isLoading={isLoading}
          emptyTitle="No users found"
          emptyDescription="Try adjusting your search or filters."
          onRowClick={(row) => router.push(`/users/${row.id}`)}
          toolbar={
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <Search size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-soft" />
                <input
                  placeholder="Search by name or email"
                  value={search}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                  className="h-10 w-full rounded-control border border-line bg-card pl-9 pr-3 text-[13px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:border-line-strong"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
                <Select placeholder="All roles" options={ROLE_OPTIONS} value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)} />
              </div>
              <div className="sm:w-[180px]">
                <Select placeholder="All statuses" options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} />
              </div>
            </div>
          }
        />
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
