"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ShieldCheck, AlertCircle, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, Input, ThemeToggle } from "@saachi/ui";
import { findStaffUser, setAuthCookies, ROLE_LABEL, type StaffRole, type StaffUser } from "../../lib/permissions";

const DEMO_CREDENTIALS: { role: StaffRole; email: string; password: string }[] = [
  { role: "super_admin", email: "superadmin@saachi.ai", password: "super123" },
  { role: "admin", email: "admin@saachi.ai", password: "admin123" },
  { role: "finance_officer", email: "finance@saachi.ai", password: "finance123" },
  { role: "support_officer", email: "support@saachi.ai", password: "support123" }
];

// Demo-only fixed code - a real backend would generate/verify this server-side (TOTP or SMS/email OTP).
const DEMO_2FA_CODE = "123456";

export default function AdminLoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<"credentials" | "2fa">("credentials");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [pendingUser, setPendingUser] = useState<StaffUser | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleCredentialsSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    const user = findStaffUser(email, password);
    if (!user) {
      setError("Invalid email or password. Check the demo credentials below.");
      return;
    }

    setPendingUser(user);
    setStep("2fa");
  };

  const handle2faSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (!pendingUser) {
      setStep("credentials");
      return;
    }

    if (code.trim() !== DEMO_2FA_CODE) {
      setError(`Invalid code. For this demo, use ${DEMO_2FA_CODE}.`);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setAuthCookies(pendingUser);
    setTimeout(() => router.push("/dashboard"), 500);
  };

  const backToCredentials = () => {
    setStep("credentials");
    setCode("");
    setError("");
  };

  const fillDemo = (cred: (typeof DEMO_CREDENTIALS)[number]) => {
    setEmail(cred.email);
    setPassword(cred.password);
    setError("");
  };

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-paper px-4 py-10">
      <div className="absolute right-5 top-5">
        <ThemeToggle />
      </div>

      <div className="flex w-full max-w-[860px] flex-col gap-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-start">
        <Card className="w-full max-w-[400px] p-8 lg:mx-auto">
          <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
            <span className="mb-4 flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-ink text-[15px] font-bold text-ink">
              S
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Saachi AI - Admin</h1>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              {step === "credentials" ? "Internal staff access only." : "Enter your verification code."}
            </p>
          </div>

          {step === "credentials" ? (
            <form onSubmit={handleCredentialsSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <Input
                label="Email"
                type="email"
                placeholder="you@saachi.ai"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                required
              />
              <Input
                label="Password"
                type="password"
                placeholder="********"
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                required
              />

              {error && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-control border border-error bg-error-tint px-3 py-2.5 text-[12.5px] text-error">
                  <AlertCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{error}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              <Button type="submit" size="lg">
                Continue
              </Button>
            </form>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handle2faSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim px-3.5 py-3 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                A 6-digit verification code has been sent to <span className="font-medium text-ink">{pendingUser?.email}</span>.
                For this demo, use <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">{DEMO_2FA_CODE}</span>.
              </div>

              <Input
                label="Verification code"
                type="text"
                inputMode="numeric"
                maxLength={6}
                placeholder="000000"
                value={code}
                onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))}
                required
                className="text-center font-mono text-[18px] tracking-[0.3em]"
              />

              {error && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-control border border-error bg-error-tint px-3 py-2.5 text-[12.5px] text-error">
                  <AlertCircle size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{error}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={loading || code.length < 6}>
                {loading ? "Verifying..." : "Verify & continue"}
              </Button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={backToCredentials}
                className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:text-ink"
              >
                <ArrowLeft size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Back to login
              </button>
            </form>
          )}

          <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            <ShieldCheck size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            Role-based access control demo - 2FA required
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card className="w-full max-w-[400px] p-6 lg:mx-auto">
          <p className="mb-1 text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">Demo credentials</p>
          <p className="mb-4 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            No backend yet - click a role below to auto-fill and try each access level. 2FA code: <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink">{DEMO_2FA_CODE}</span>.
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {DEMO_CREDENTIALS.map((cred) => (
              <button
                key={cred.role}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fillDemo(cred)}
                disabled={step === "2fa"}
                className="flex flex-col items-start rounded-control border border-line px-3.5 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-line-strong hover:bg-paper-dim disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{ROLE_LABEL[cred.role]}</span>
                <span className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-soft">{cred.email}</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-soft">{cred.password}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

  role: StaffRole;
};

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto flex max-w-[600px] flex-col items-center px-4 py-24 text-center">
      <div className="mb-5 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-error text-error">

        permission to view this page. Switch to a role with access, or contact a Super Admin if you believe this is
        a mistake.

FILE: apps\admin\app\_components\AdminShell.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import {
  Menu,
  X,
  LogOut,
  Search,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Bell,
  User,
  Settings,
  ChevronDown,
  Command,
  Check
} from "lucide-react";
import { ThemeToggle } from "@saachi/ui";
import { ADMIN_NAV } from "../../lib/nav-config";
import {
  ROLE_LABEL,
  getAllowedRolesForRoute,
  isRouteAllowed,
  getCurrentRole,
  getCurrentName,
  getCurrentEmail,
  setCurrentRole,
  clearAuthCookies,
  type StaffRole
} from "../../lib/permissions";
import { CommandPalette } from "./CommandPalette";
import { AccessDenied } from "./AccessDenied";

function cx(...parts: Array<string | false | undefined>) {
  return parts.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

type NotificationItem = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  tone: "warning" | "error" | "info";
  href: string;
};

const NOTIFICATIONS: NotificationItem[] = [
  { id: "n1", label: "3 courses pending moderation review", tone: "warning", href: "/courses/moderation" },
  { id: "n2", label: "1 refund request awaiting approval", tone: "error", href: "/finance/refunds" },
  { id: "n3", label: "2 support tickets breaching SLA", tone: "error", href: "/support/tickets" },
  { id: "n4", label: "Weekly payout batch ready to process", tone: "info", href: "/finance/payouts" }
];

const TONE_DOT: Record<NotificationItem["tone"], string> = {
  warning: "bg-warning",
  error: "bg-error",
  info: "bg-info"
};

const ROLE_LIST: StaffRole[] = ["super_admin", "admin", "finance_officer", "support_officer"];

export function AdminShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [notifOpen, setNotifOpen] = useState(false);
  const [userMenuOpen, setUserMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [currentRole, setCurrentRoleState] = useState<StaffRole>("super_admin");
  const [currentName, setCurrentName] = useState("Abdul Rehman Irfan");
  const [currentEmail, setCurrentEmail] = useState("abdulrehman@saachi.ai");
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  const notifRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const userMenuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentRoleState(getCurrentRole());
    setCurrentName(getCurrentName());
    setCurrentEmail(getCurrentEmail());
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (notifRef.current && !notifRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setNotifOpen(false);
      if (userMenuRef.current && !userMenuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setUserMenuOpen(false);
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const handleLogout = () => {
    clearAuthCookies();
    router.push("/login");
  };

  const handleSwitchRole = (role: StaffRole) => {
    setCurrentRoleState(role);
    setCurrentRole(role);
    setUserMenuOpen(false);
    toast.success(`Switched to ${ROLE_LABEL[role]}`, {
      description: "Your view now reflects this role's permissions."
    });
    if (!isRouteAllowed(pathname ?? "/dashboard", role)) {
      router.push("/dashboard");
    }
  };

  const filteredNav = ADMIN_NAV.map((section) => ({
    ...section,
    items: section.items.filter((item) => getAllowedRolesForRoute(item.href).includes(currentRole))
  })).filter((section) => section.items.length > 0);

  const sidebarContent = (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col pt-[65px]">
      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-5 overflow-y-auto px-3 pb-4 pt-4">
        {filteredNav.map((navSection) => (
          <div key={navSection.title}>
            {!collapsed && (
              <div className="mb-1.5 px-2.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink-soft/60">
                {navSection.title}
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
              {navSection.items.map((item) => {
                const active = pathname === item.href || Boolean(pathname?.startsWith(`${item.href}/`));
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={item.href}
                    href={item.href}
                    onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                    title={collapsed ? item.label : undefined}
                    className={cx(
                      "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                      collapsed && "justify-center",
                      active ? "bg-accent text-paper" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                    )}
                  >
                    <item.icon size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0" />
                    {!collapsed && <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>}
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </nav>

      <div className="border-t border-line px-3 py-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setCollapsed((v) => !v)}
          className={cx(
            "mb-1 flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink",
            collapsed && "justify-center"
          )}
        >
          {collapsed ? <ChevronRight size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} /> : <ChevronLeft size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />}
          {!collapsed && "Collapse"}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleLogout}
          className={cx(
            "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-error",
            collapsed && "justify-center"
          )}
        >
          <LogOut size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          {!collapsed && "Log out"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  const allowed = mounted ? isRouteAllowed(pathname ?? "/dashboard", currentRole) : true;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-paper">
      <CommandPalette />

      <div
        className={cx(
          "hidden shrink-0 border-r border-line bg-card transition-[width] duration-200 ease-out md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:flex",
          collapsed ? "w-[76px]" : "w-[248px]"
        )}
      >
        {sidebarContent}
      </div>

      <div className="sticky top-0 z-40 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-line bg-card/95 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur-sm md:px-6">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
            aria-label="Open menu"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink md:hidden"
          >
            <Menu size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
          </button>

          <div className="hidden shrink-0 items-center gap-2 sm:flex">
            <span className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-ink text-[12px] font-bold text-ink">
              S
            </span>
            <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Saachi Admin</span>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden h-6 w-px shrink-0 bg-line sm:block" />

          <div className="relative min-w-0" ref={userMenuRef}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen((v) => !v)}
              className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-control px-1.5 py-1 transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
            >
              <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink">
                {currentName.charAt(0)}
              </div>
              <div className="hidden min-w-0 text-left sm:block">
                <div className="truncate text-[12.5px] font-semibold leading-tight text-ink">{currentName}</div>
                <div className="truncate text-[11px] leading-tight text-ink-soft">{ROLE_LABEL[currentRole]}</div>
              </div>
              <ChevronDown size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} className="hidden shrink-0 text-ink-soft sm:block" />
            </button>

            {userMenuOpen && (
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] w-[260px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg">
                <div className="border-b border-line px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">{currentName}</p>
                  <p className="truncate text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{currentEmail}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="border-b border-line px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="mb-2 text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft/70">
                    Switch role (testing)
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                    {ROLE_LIST.map((role) => (
                      <button
                        key={role}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleSwitchRole(role)}
                        className={cx(
                          "flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-control px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-[12.5px] transition-colors duration-100 ease-out",
                          role === currentRole ? "bg-paper-dim text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                        )}
                      >
                        {ROLE_LABEL[role]}
                        {role === currentRole && <Check size={13} strokeWidth={2} className="shrink-0 text-ink" />}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col p-1.5">
                  {getAllowedRolesForRoute("/settings/roles").includes(currentRole) && (
                    <Link
                      href="/settings/roles"
                      onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                    >
                      <User size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Profile
                    </Link>
                  )}
                  {getAllowedRolesForRoute("/settings/platform").includes(currentRole) && (
                    <Link
                      href="/settings/platform"
                      onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(false)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                    >
                      <Settings size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Settings
                    </Link>
                  )}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleLogout}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-left text-[13px] text-error transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-error-tint"
                  >
                    <LogOut size={14} strokeWidth={1.75} /> Log out
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "k", metaKey: true }))}
            className="hidden items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:border-line-strong hover:text-ink sm:flex"
          >
            <Search size={13} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            <span>Search</span>
            <span className="ml-1.5 flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded border border-line-strong px-1 py-0.5 text-[10px]">
              <Command size={9} strokeWidth={2} />K
            </span>
          </button>

          <div className="relative" ref={notifRef}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setNotifOpen((v) => !v)}
              aria-label="Notifications"
              className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
            >
              <Bell size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
              {NOTIFICATIONS.length > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute right-1.5 top-1.5 flex h-4 min-w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-error px-1 text-[9px] font-semibold text-paper">
                  {NOTIFICATIONS.length}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>

            {notifOpen && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] w-[320px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg">
                <div className="border-b border-line px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink">Notifications</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col">
                  {NOTIFICATIONS.map((n) => (
                    <Link
                      key={n.id}
                      href={n.href}
                      onClick={() => setNotifOpen(false)}
                      className="flex items-start gap-2.5 border-b border-line px-4 py-3 text-[12.5px] text-ink transition-colors duration-100 ease-out last:border-0 hover:bg-paper-dim"
                    >
                      <span className={cx("mt-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full", TONE_DOT[n.tone])} />
                      <span>{n.label}</span>
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <ThemeToggle />
        </div>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex md:hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/40 transition-opacity duration-200 ease-out" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />
          <div className="relative flex h-full w-[280px] flex-col bg-card transition-transform duration-200 ease-out">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
              aria-label="Close menu"
              className="absolute right-3 top-3 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink"
            >
              <X size={18} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            </button>
            {sidebarContent}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className={cx("transition-[padding] duration-200 ease-out", collapsed ? "md:pl-[76px]" : "md:pl-[248px]")}>
        {allowed ? children : <AccessDenied role={currentRole} />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: apps\admin\app\_components\CommandPalette.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search, CornerDownLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { ADMIN_NAV } from "../../lib/nav-config";

type FlatItem = {
  label: string;
  href: string;
  section: string;
  icon: (typeof ADMIN_NAV)[number]["items"][number]["icon"];
};

export function CommandPalette() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);

  const flatItems: FlatItem[] = useMemo(
    () =>
      ADMIN_NAV.flatMap((section) =>
        section.items.map((item) => ({
          label: item.label,
          href: item.href,
          section: section.title,
          icon: item.icon
        }))
      ),
    []
  );

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    if (!query.trim()) return flatItems;
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return flatItems.filter(
      (item) => item.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) || item.section.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [flatItems, query]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const isCmdK = (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k";
      if (isCmdK) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setOpen((v) => !v);
      }
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handler);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setQuery("");
      setActiveIndex(0);
    }
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveIndex(0);
  }, [query]);

  const navigate = (href: string) => {
    router.push(href);
    setOpen(false);
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
      e.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex((i) => Math.min(i + 1, filtered.length - 1));
    } else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
      e.preventDefault();
      setActiveIndex((i) => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
    } else if (e.key === "Enter") {
      e.preventDefault();
      const target = filtered[activeIndex];
      if (target) navigate(target.href);
    }
  };

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-start justify-center bg-ink/40 px-4 pt-[12vh]">
      <div className="absolute inset-0" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} />
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-[560px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-line px-4 py-3">
          <Search size={16} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
          <input
            autoFocus
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
            placeholder="Jump to a page... (Users, Payouts, Settings)"
            className="h-6 w-full bg-transparent text-[14px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60"
          />
          <kbd className="hidden shrink-0 rounded border border-line-strong px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-ink-soft sm:block">
            Esc
          </kbd>
        </div>

        <div className="max-h-[360px] overflow-y-auto p-2">
          {filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-3 py-6 text-center text-[13px] text-ink-soft">No matching pages.</p>
          ) : (
            filtered.map((item, index) => (
              <button
                key={item.href}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => navigate(item.href)}
                onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIndex(index)}
                className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-[13px] transition-colors duration-100 ease-out ${
                  index === activeIndex ? "bg-accent text-paper" : "text-ink hover:bg-paper-dim"
                }`}
              >
                <item.icon size={15} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0" />
                <span className="flex-1 truncate">{item.label}</span>
                <span
                  className={`shrink-0 text-[11px] ${index === activeIndex ? "text-paper/70" : "text-ink-soft/60"}`}
                >
                  {item.section}
                </span>
                {index === activeIndex && <CornerDownLeft size={12} strokeWidth={1.75} className="shrink-0" />}
              </button>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Lottie from "lottie-react";
import animationData from "../../public/animations/loading.json";

type LoadingSpinnerProps = {
  size?: number;
  label?: string;
  fullScreen?: boolean;
};

export function LoadingSpinner({ size = 72, label, fullScreen = false }: LoadingSpinnerProps) {
  const content = (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
      <div style={{ width: size, height: size }}>
        <Lottie animationData={animationData} loop autoplay />
      </div>
      {label && <p className="text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{label}</p>}
    </div>
  );

  if (fullScreen) {
    return <div className="flex min-h-[60vh] w-full items-center justify-center">{content}</div>;
  }

  return content;
}

export function InlineSpinner({ size = 18 }: { size?: number }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ width: size, height: size }} className="inline-block">
      <Lottie animationData={animationData} loop autoplay />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\admin\app\providers.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Toaster } from "sonner";

export function Providers({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [queryClient] = useState(
    () =>
      new QueryClient({
        defaultOptions: {
          queries: {
            staleTime: 30 * 1000,
            refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
            retry: 1
          }
        }
      })
  );

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      {children}
      <Toaster
        position="top-right"
        closeButton
        toastOptions={{
          style: {
            background: "var(--color-card)",
            color: "var(--color-ink)",
            border: "1px solid var(--color-line)",
            borderRadius: "10px",
            fontSize: "13px"
          }
        }}
      />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

// Maps each dashboard widget (KPI, quick action, chart, notification, activity type)
// to the route-permission prefix that governs it. Dashboard content is derived from
// this file + canAccessModule() so it always stays in sync with the sidebar/route
// permission matrix in lib/permissions.ts - no per-role dashboard hardcoding.

export type DashboardModuleKey =
  | "/users"
  | "/finance"
  | "/courses"
  | "/support"
  | "/analytics"
  | "/marketing"
  | "/settings/commission-tiers";

export const KPI_MODULE_MAP: Record<string, DashboardModuleKey> = {
  signups: "/users",
  revenue: "/finance",
  approvals: "/courses",
  tickets: "/support"
};

export const QUICK_ACTION_MODULE_MAP: Record<string, DashboardModuleKey> = {
  approve_queue: "/courses",
  process_payouts: "/finance",
  view_tickets: "/support",
  export_report: "/analytics"
};

export const NOTIFICATION_MODULE_MAP: Record<string, DashboardModuleKey> = {
  n1: "/courses",   // moderation queue
  n2: "/finance",   // refund request
  n3: "/support",   // SLA breach
  n4: "/finance"    // payout batch
};

export const ACTIVITY_TYPE_MODULE_MAP: Record<string, DashboardModuleKey> = {
  transaction: "/finance",
  course: "/courses",
  ticket: "/support",
  user: "/users"
};

// Revenue/enrollment/pie charts are grouped under "/finance" and "/analytics"
// respectively since that's what governs their visibility per the permission matrix.
export const REVENUE_CHART_MODULE: DashboardModuleKey = "/finance";
export const PIE_CHART_MODULE: DashboardModuleKey = "/finance";
export const ENROLLMENT_CHART_MODULE: DashboardModuleKey = "/analytics";

FILE: apps\admin\lib\permissions.ts
================================================================================
export type StaffRole = "super_admin" | "admin" | "finance_officer" | "support_officer";

export type StaffUser = {
  email: string;
  password: string;
  role: StaffRole;
  name: string;
};

export const ROLE_LABEL: Record<StaffRole, string> = {
  super_admin: "Super Admin",
  admin: "Admin",
  finance_officer: "Finance Officer",
  support_officer: "Support Officer"
};

// Dummy staff directory - no backend yet, credentials are hardcoded for demo/testing.
export const STAFF_USERS: StaffUser[] = [
  { email: "superadmin@saachi.ai", password: "super123", role: "super_admin", name: "Abdul Rehman Irfan" },
  { email: "admin@saachi.ai", password: "admin123", role: "admin", name: "Admin User" },
  { email: "finance@saachi.ai", password: "finance123", role: "finance_officer", name: "Finance Desk" },
  { email: "support@saachi.ai", password: "support123", role: "support_officer", name: "Support Desk" }
];

export function findStaffUser(email: string, password: string): StaffUser | null {
  const match = STAFF_USERS.find(
    (u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === email.trim().toLowerCase() && u.password === password
  );
  return match ?? null;
}

// Route prefix -> roles allowed to access it. Checked in order, first match wins.
// Any route not listed here is allowed for all roles (e.g. /dashboard).
const ROUTE_PERMISSIONS: { prefix: string; roles: StaffRole[] }[] = [
  // Settings - Super Admin only
  { prefix: "/settings/roles", roles: ["super_admin"] },
  { prefix: "/settings/commission-tiers", roles: ["super_admin"] },
  { prefix: "/settings/payment-gateways", roles: ["super_admin"] },
  { prefix: "/settings/platform", roles: ["super_admin"] },
  { prefix: "/settings/legal-documents", roles: ["super_admin"] },

  // Finance - Super Admin, Admin, Finance Officer
  { prefix: "/finance", roles: ["super_admin", "admin", "finance_officer"] },

  // Analytics - Super Admin, Admin, Finance Officer
  { prefix: "/analytics", roles: ["super_admin", "admin", "finance_officer"] },

  // Courses - Super Admin, Admin only
  { prefix: "/courses", roles: ["super_admin", "admin"] },

  // Marketing - Super Admin, Admin only
  { prefix: "/marketing", roles: ["super_admin", "admin"] },

  // Audit log - Super Admin, Admin only
  { prefix: "/logs/audit", roles: ["super_admin", "admin"] },

  // Users - Super Admin, Admin, Support Officer
  { prefix: "/users", roles: ["super_admin", "admin", "support_officer"] },

  // Moderation (reviews, forum, content) - Super Admin, Admin, Support Officer
  { prefix: "/moderation", roles: ["super_admin", "admin", "support_officer"] },

  // Support tickets - Super Admin, Admin, Support Officer
  { prefix: "/support", roles: ["super_admin", "admin", "support_officer"] }

  // /dashboard and anything else not listed above -> allowed for all roles
];

export function isRouteAllowed(pathname: string, role: StaffRole): boolean {
  const rule = ROUTE_PERMISSIONS.find((r) => pathname === r.prefix || pathname.startsWith(`${r.prefix}/`));
  if (!rule) return true; // not restricted -> everyone can access
  return rule.roles.includes(role);
}

export function getAllowedRolesForRoute(prefix: string): StaffRole[] {
  const rule = ROUTE_PERMISSIONS.find((r) => prefix === r.prefix || prefix.startsWith(`${r.prefix}/`) || r.prefix.startsWith(prefix));
  if (!rule) return ["super_admin", "admin", "finance_officer", "support_officer"];
  return rule.roles;
}

// ---- Cookie helpers (client-side, no backend) ----

const SESSION_COOKIE = "saachi_admin_session";
const ROLE_COOKIE = "saachi_admin_role";
const NAME_COOKIE = "saachi_admin_name";
const EMAIL_COOKIE = "saachi_admin_email";

export function setAuthCookies(user: StaffUser) {
  const maxAge = 60 * 60 * 24 * 7; // 7 days
  document.cookie = `${SESSION_COOKIE}=demo_token; path=/; max-age=${maxAge}`;
  document.cookie = `${ROLE_COOKIE}=${user.role}; path=/; max-age=${maxAge}`;
  document.cookie = `${NAME_COOKIE}=${encodeURIComponent(user.name)}; path=/; max-age=${maxAge}`;
  document.cookie = `${EMAIL_COOKIE}=${encodeURIComponent(user.email)}; path=/; max-age=${maxAge}`;
}

export function clearAuthCookies() {
  document.cookie = `${SESSION_COOKIE}=; path=/; max-age=0`;
  document.cookie = `${ROLE_COOKIE}=; path=/; max-age=0`;
  document.cookie = `${NAME_COOKIE}=; path=/; max-age=0`;
  document.cookie = `${EMAIL_COOKIE}=; path=/; max-age=0`;
}

function readCookie(name: string): string | null {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return null;
  const match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp(`(?:^|; )${name}=([^;]*)`));
  return match ? decodeURIComponent(match[1]) : null;
}

export function getCurrentRole(): StaffRole {
  const stored = readCookie(ROLE_COOKIE) as StaffRole | null;
  const valid: StaffRole[] = ["super_admin", "admin", "finance_officer", "support_officer"];
  return stored && valid.includes(stored) ? stored : "super_admin";
}

export function getCurrentName(): string {
  return readCookie(NAME_COOKIE) ?? "Abdul Rehman Irfan";
}

export function getCurrentEmail(): string {
  return readCookie(EMAIL_COOKIE) ?? "abdulrehman@saachi.ai";
}

export function setCurrentRole(role: StaffRole) {
  const maxAge = 60 * 60 * 24 * 7;
  document.cookie = `${ROLE_COOKIE}=${role}; path=/; max-age=${maxAge}`;
}

// ---- Module-level access check (used by dashboard widgets, quick actions, charts) ----
// Reuses the same ROUTE_PERMISSIONS matrix as the sidebar/route guard, so there is a
// single source of truth for "who can see what."
export function canAccessModule(modulePrefix: string, role: StaffRole): boolean {
  return getAllowedRolesForRoute(modulePrefix).includes(role);
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\admin\middleware.ts
================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

const PUBLIC_PATHS = ["/login"];

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const adminSession = request.cookies.get("saachi_admin_session");

  const isPublic = PUBLIC_PATHS.some((p) => pathname.startsWith(p));

  if (!isPublic && !adminSession) {
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)"]
};

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\forgot-password\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend password-reset-request mutation wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSubmitting(false);
      setSubmitted(true);
    }, 500);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-md px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
        <EmptyState
          title="Check your email"
          description={`If an account exists for ${email}, we've sent a link to reset your password.`}
          action={
            <Link href="/login">
              <Button variant="secondary">Back to login</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Forgot password</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Enter your email and we&apos;ll send you a link to reset your password.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="you@example.com"
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={submitting}>
          Send reset link
        </Button>
      </form>

      <p className="mt-8 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Remembered your password?{" "}
        <Link href="/login" className="font-semibold text-gold-dark hover:underline">
          Log in
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\login\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, SocialLoginButtons } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useLogin } from "@saachi/api-client";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const login = useLogin();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    login.mutate(
      { email, password },
      {
        onSuccess: (session) =>
          router.push(session.role === "instructor" ? "/instructor/dashboard" : "/dashboard")
      }
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Log in</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Welcome back to Saachi AI.</p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="you@example.com"
        />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={login.isPending}>
          Log in
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <SocialLoginButtons />
      </div>

      <p className="mt-8 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Don&apos;t have an account?{" "}
        <Link href="/register" className="font-semibold text-gold-dark hover:underline">
          Sign up
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\register\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, Select, SocialLoginButtons } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useRegister } from "@saachi/api-client";

const ROLE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Learner - I want to learn AI skills", value: "learner" },
  { label: "Instructor - I want to teach and sell courses", value: "instructor" }
];

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const register = useRegister();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("learner");

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (password !== confirmPassword) return;
    register.mutate(
      { fullName, email, password, confirmPassword, role: role as "learner" | "instructor" },
      { onSuccess: () => router.push(role === "instructor" ? "/instructor/dashboard" : "/dashboard") }
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Create your account</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Join the Saachi AI marketplace.</p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="Full name"
          required
          value={fullName}
          onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Your full name"
        />
        <Input
          label="Email"
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="you@example.com"
        />
        <Select label="I am joining as" options={ROLE_OPTIONS} value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)} />
        <Input
          label="Password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
        />
        <Input
          label="Confirm password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={confirmPassword}
          onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
          error={confirmPassword && password !== confirmPassword ? "Passwords do not match" : undefined}
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={register.isPending}>
          Create account
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-6">
        <SocialLoginButtons />
      </div>

      <p className="mt-8 text-center text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Already have an account?{" "}
        <Link href="/login" className="font-semibold text-gold-dark hover:underline">
          Log in
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\reset-password\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent, Suspense } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function ResetPasswordForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const token = searchParams.get("token");

  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (password !== confirmPassword) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend password-reset-confirm mutation wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSubmitting(false);
      setDone(true);
    }, 500);
  }

  if (!token) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="Invalid or expired link"
        description="This password reset link is missing or no longer valid."
        action={
          <Link href="/forgot-password">
            <Button>Request a new link</Button>
          </Link>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  if (done) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="Password updated"
        description="Your password has been reset. You can now log in."
        action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/login")}>Go to login</Button>}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Reset password</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Choose a new password for your account.</p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Input
          label="New password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="At least 8 characters"
        />
        <Input
          label="Confirm new password"
          type="password"
          required
          value={confirmPassword}
          onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
          error={confirmPassword && password !== confirmPassword ? "Passwords do not match" : undefined}
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={submitting}>
          Reset password
        </Button>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <ResetPasswordForm />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(auth)\verify-email\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Suspense, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { MailCheck, MailWarning } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Loading, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function VerifyEmailContent() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const token = searchParams.get("token");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"checking" | "success" | "error">("checking");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!token) {
      setStatus("error");
      return;
    }
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend email-verification mutation wired up yet.
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setStatus("success"), 800);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [token]);

  if (status === "checking") {
    return <Loading label="Verifying your email..." />;
  }

  if (status === "error") {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="Verification link invalid"
        description="This email verification link is missing or has expired."
        action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/login")}>Back to login</Button>}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
      <div className="mb-4 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-light text-teal">
        <MailCheck size={26} />
      </div>
      <h1 className="mb-2 font-display text-[24px] font-bold text-ink">Email verified</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 max-w-sm text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Your email address has been confirmed. You can now access your account.
      </p>
      <Button onClick={() => router.push("/dashboard")}>Go to dashboard</Button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function VerifyEmailPage() {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col px-4 py-20 md:px-0">
      <Suspense
        fallback={
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center text-ink-soft">
            <MailWarning size={26} className="mb-3" />
            Loading...
          </div>
        }
      >
        <VerifyEmailContent />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, Select, Textarea, EmptyState, cn } from "@saachi/ui";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../../../../lib/categories";

const STEPS = ["Basics", "Curriculum", "Pricing", "Media", "Review & Submit"];

const LEVEL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Beginner", value: "beginner" },
  { label: "Intermediate", value: "intermediate" },
  { label: "Advanced", value: "advanced" }
];

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "English", value: "en" },
  { label: "Urdu", value: "ur" }
];

type LessonType = "video" | "text" | "quiz" | "resource";

type Lesson = { id: string; title: string; type: LessonType };
type Module = { id: string; title: string; lessons: Lesson[] };

let idCounter = 0;
function nextId() {
  idCounter += 1;
  return `m${idCounter}`;
}

export default function CourseWizardPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  // Step 1: Basics
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [subtitle, setSubtitle] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(CATEGORIES[0]?.id ?? "");
  const [level, setLevel] = useState("beginner");
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState("en");

  // Step 2: Curriculum
  const [modules, setModules] = useState<Module[]>([]);

  function addModule() {
    setModules((prev) => [...prev, { id: nextId(), title: `Module ${prev.length + 1}`, lessons: [] }]);
  }

  function addLesson(moduleId: string, type: LessonType) {
    setModules((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) =>
        m.id === moduleId
          ? { ...m, lessons: [...m.lessons, { id: nextId(), title: `New ${type} lesson`, type }] }
          : m
      )
    );
  }

  // Step 3: Pricing
  const [price, setPrice] = useState("");
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState("USD");
  const [includeInSubscription, setIncludeInSubscription] = useState(false);

  // Step 4: Media
  const [thumbnailUrl, setThumbnailUrl] = useState("");
  const [promoVideoUrl, setPromoVideoUrl] = useState("");

  const canGoNext = (() => {
    if (step === 0) return title.trim().length > 0 && category.length > 0;
    if (step === 1) return modules.length > 0;
    if (step === 2) return price.trim().length > 0;
    return true;
  })();

  function handleSubmit() {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend course-creation mutation wired up yet.
    setSubmitted(true);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg py-16">
        <EmptyState
          title="Course submitted for review"
          description="Our moderation team will review your course and notify you once it's approved."
          action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/instructor/courses")}>Back to my courses</Button>}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Create a new course</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Step {step + 1} of {STEPS.length}: {STEPS[step]}</p>

      {/* Stepper */}
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-2">
        {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
          <div key={s} className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2">
            <div
              className={cn(
                "flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold",
                i < step ? "bg-teal text-white" : i === step ? "bg-gold-dark text-white" : "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft"
              )}
            >
              {i < step ? <Check size={14} /> : i + 1}
            </div>
            {i < STEPS.length - 1 && <div className={cn("h-0.5 flex-1", i < step ? "bg-teal" : "bg-line")} />}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        {step === 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <Input label="Course title" required value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. n8n Automation Bootcamp" />
            <Input label="Subtitle" value={subtitle} onChange={(e) => setSubtitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="A short one-line summary" />
            <Select
              label="Category"
              options={CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id }))}
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Select label="Level" options={LEVEL_OPTIONS} value={level} onChange={(e) => setLevel(e.target.value)} />
              <Select label="Language" options={LANGUAGE_OPTIONS} value={language} onChange={(e) => setLanguage(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 1 && (
          <div>
            {modules.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState title="No modules yet" description="Add your first module to start building the curriculum." />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                {modules.map((m) => (
                  <div key={m.id} className="rounded-control border border-line p-4">
                    <p className="mb-3 text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.title}</p>
                    {m.lessons.length > 0 && (
                      <ul className="mb-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                        {m.lessons.map((l) => (
                          <li key={l.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                            <span className="rounded bg-paper-dim px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] uppercase">{l.type}</span>
                            {l.title}
                          </li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    )}
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "video")}>+ Video</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "text")}>+ Text</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "quiz")}>+ Quiz</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => addLesson(m.id, "resource")}>+ Resource</Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
            <Button variant="secondary" className="mt-4" onClick={addModule}>+ Add module</Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 2 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Input label="Price" type="number" required value={price} onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)} placeholder="49.00" />
              <Select
                label="Currency"
                options={[
                  { label: "USD", value: "USD" },
                  { label: "PKR", value: "PKR" }
                ]}
                value={currency}
                onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={includeInSubscription}
                onChange={(e) => setIncludeInSubscription(e.target.checked)}
                className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
              />
              Include this course in the Saachi AI Pro subscription
            </label>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 3 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <Input label="Thumbnail URL" value={thumbnailUrl} onChange={(e) => setThumbnailUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://..." />
            <Input label="Promo video URL" value={promoVideoUrl} onChange={(e) => setPromoVideoUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://..." />
            <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
              File upload with resumable/chunked upload will be wired up once the signed-URL flow from the backend is available.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 4 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 text-[14px]">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Title</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{title || "â€”"}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Category</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.id === category)?.name ?? "â€”"}</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Modules</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{modules.length} module(s), {modules.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m.lessons.length, 0)} lesson(s)</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.05em] text-ink-soft/60">Price</p>
              <p className="text-ink">{price ? `${price} ${currency}` : "â€”"}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-between">
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={step === 0} onClick={() => setStep((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1))}>
          Back
        </Button>
        {step < STEPS.length - 1 ? (
          <Button disabled={!canGoNext} onClick={() => setStep((s) => Math.min(STEPS.length - 1, s + 1))}>
            Next
          </Button>
        ) : (
          <Button onClick={handleSubmit}>Submit for review</Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button, EmptyState, Select, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

type CourseStatus = "draft" | "pending_review" | "published" | "rejected" | "archived";

type InstructorCourse = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  status: CourseStatus;
  enrollments: number;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorCourses() once
// packages/api-client's instructor-course hooks are confirmed.
const COURSES: InstructorCourse[] = [];

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "All", value: "all" },
  { label: "Draft", value: "draft" },
  { label: "Pending review", value: "pending_review" },
  { label: "Published", value: "published" },
  { label: "Rejected", value: "rejected" },
  { label: "Archived", value: "archived" }
];

// NOTE: only using Badge variants confirmed to exist ("gold" | "success" | "error").
// Draft/archived fall back to no variant prop (component default styling).
const STATUS_VARIANT: Partial<Record<CourseStatus, "gold" | "success" | "error">> = {
  pending_review: "gold",
  published: "success",
  rejected: "error"
};

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<CourseStatus, string> = {
  draft: "Draft",
  pending_review: "Pending review",
  published: "Published",
  rejected: "Rejected",
  archived: "Archived"
};

export default function InstructorMyCoursesPage() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");

  const filtered = COURSES.filter((c) => status === "all" || c.status === status);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">My Courses</h1>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Select options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} className="w-48" />
          <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
            <Button>New course</Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No courses yet"
          description="Create your first course to start selling on Saachi AI."
          action={
            <Link href="/instructor/courses/new">
              <Button>Create a course</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
            <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Course</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Status</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Enrollments</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Price</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((c) => (
                <tr key={c.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-medium text-ink">{c.title}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    {STATUS_VARIANT[c.status] ? (
                      <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANT[c.status]}>{STATUS_LABEL[c.status]}</Badge>
                    ) : (
                      <Badge>{STATUS_LABEL[c.status]}</Badge>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{c.enrollments}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-mono text-ink">
                    {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: c.currency }).format(c.price)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <Link href={`/instructor/courses/${c.id}/edit`} className="text-gold-dark hover:underline">
                      Edit
                    </Link>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\mentorship\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { CalendarDays, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, Select, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type Slot = { id: string; day: string; time: string };

const DAY_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Monday", value: "Mon" },
  { label: "Tuesday", value: "Tue" },
  { label: "Wednesday", value: "Wed" },
  { label: "Thursday", value: "Thu" },
  { label: "Friday", value: "Fri" },
  { label: "Saturday", value: "Sat" },
  { label: "Sunday", value: "Sun" }
];

let slotCounter = 0;

export default function InstructorMentorshipSettingsPage() {
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [hourlyRate, setHourlyRate] = useState("40");
  const [sessionLength, setSessionLength] = useState("30");
  const [day, setDay] = useState("Mon");
  const [time, setTime] = useState("");
  const [slots, setSlots] = useState<Slot[]>([]);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  function addSlot() {
    if (!time.trim()) return;
    slotCounter += 1;
    setSlots((prev) => [...prev, { id: `slot${slotCounter}`, day, time }]);
    setTime("");
  }

  function removeSlot(id: string) {
    setSlots((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== id));
  }

  function handleSave() {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend mentorship-settings mutation wired up yet.
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 3000);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Mentorship Settings</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Offer paid 1-on-1 mentorship sessions to learners.</p>

      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
        <div>
          <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Enable mentorship</p>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Allow learners to book paid sessions with you.</p>
        </div>
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={enabled}
          onChange={(e) => setEnabled(e.target.checked)}
          aria-label="Toggle mentorship availability"
          className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
        />
      </div>

      {enabled && (
        <>
          <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Input label="Hourly rate (USD)" type="number" value={hourlyRate} onChange={(e) => setHourlyRate(e.target.value)} />
            <Select
              label="Session length (minutes)"
              options={[
                { label: "30 minutes", value: "30" },
                { label: "45 minutes", value: "45" },
                { label: "60 minutes", value: "60" }
              ]}
              value={sessionLength}
              onChange={(e) => setSessionLength(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
            <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
              <CalendarDays size={16} />
              Weekly availability
            </h2>

            {slots.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState title="No availability set" description="Add time slots when learners can book you." />
            ) : (
              <ul className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
                {slots.map((s) => (
                  <li key={s.id} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-control bg-paper-dim px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink">
                    {s.day} â€” {s.time}
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => removeSlot(s.id)} aria-label="Remove slot" className="text-ink-soft hover:text-error">
                      <Trash2 size={14} />
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2">
              <Select options={DAY_OPTIONS} value={day} onChange={(e) => setDay(e.target.value)} className="w-36" />
              <Input value={time} onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. 10:00 AM" className="w-40" />
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={addSlot}>
                <Plus size={14} className="mr-1.5" />
                Add slot
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <Button size="lg" className="mt-6" onClick={handleSave}>
        {saved ? "Saved" : "Save settings"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\promotions\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Rocket, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Select, EmptyState, Badge, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

type PackageTier = "bronze" | "silver" | "gold";

const PACKAGES: {
  tier: PackageTier;
  name: string;
  price: string;
  perks: string[];
}[] = [
  { tier: "bronze", name: "Bronze Boost", price: "$19", perks: ["Category page pinning (7 days)", "Basic visibility badge"] },
  { tier: "silver", name: "Silver Boost", price: "$49", perks: ["Everything in Bronze", "Top-of-search placement (7 days)", "Silver visibility badge"] },
  { tier: "gold", name: "Gold Boost", price: "$99", perks: ["Everything in Silver", "Homepage featured slot (7 days)", "Gold visibility badge"] }
];

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorCourses() once confirmed.
const COURSE_OPTIONS = [{ label: "Select a course...", value: "" }];

export default function InstructorPromotionsPage() {
  const [selectedCourse, setSelectedCourse] = useState("");
  const [purchasing, setPurchasing] = useState<PackageTier | null>(null);
  const [purchased, setPurchased] = useState<PackageTier | null>(null);

  function handlePurchase(tier: PackageTier) {
    if (!selectedCourse) return;
    setPurchasing(tier);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend promotion-purchase mutation wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setPurchasing(null);
      setPurchased(tier);
    }, 700);
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Promotions</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Boost your course visibility with a promotion package.</p>

      <div className="mb-8 max-w-sm">
        <Select
          label="Course to promote"
          options={COURSE_OPTIONS}
          value={selectedCourse}
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedCourse(e.target.value)}
        />
        {COURSE_OPTIONS.length === 1 && (
          <p className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
            You don&apos;t have any published courses to promote yet.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-3">
        {PACKAGES.map((pkg) => (
          <div
            key={pkg.tier}
            className={cn(
              "flex flex-col rounded-card border p-6",
              pkg.tier === "gold" ? "border-gold-dark/50 bg-gold-tint" : "border-line bg-card"
            )}
          >
            {pkg.tier === "gold" && (
              <Badge variant="gold" className="mb-3 self-start">
                Best value
              </Badge>
            )}
            <h2 className="font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">{pkg.name}</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[24px] font-bold text-ink">{pkg.price}</p>
            <ul className="my-5 flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              {pkg.perks.map((perk) => (
                <li key={perk} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <Check size={14} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-teal" />
                  {perk}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            <Button
              variant={pkg.tier === "gold" ? "primary" : "secondary"}
              disabled={!selectedCourse || purchasing !== null}
              onClick={() => handlePurchase(pkg.tier)}
              className="w-full"
            >
              <Rocket size={14} className="mr-1.5" />
              {purchased === pkg.tier ? "Purchased" : purchasing === pkg.tier ? "Processing..." : "Boost course"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <h2 className="mb-4 mt-12 font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Active promotions</h2>
      <EmptyState title="No active promotions" description="Purchased boost packages will appear here." />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\reviews\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Star } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Textarea, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type Review = {
  id: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  author: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  reply?: string;
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorReviews() once confirmed.
const REVIEWS: Review[] = [];

export default function InstructorReviewsPage() {
  const [replies, setReplies] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [submittedIds, setSubmittedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

  function handleReplyChange(id: string, value: string) {
    setReplies((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: value }));
  }

  function handleSubmitReply(id: string) {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend review-reply mutation wired up yet.
    setSubmittedIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(id));
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Reviews</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">View and respond to reviews on your courses.</p>

      {REVIEWS.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No reviews yet" description="Reviews from learners on your courses will appear here." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {REVIEWS.map((r) => (
            <div key={r.id} className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
              <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{r.courseTitle}</p>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-gold-dark">
                  {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                    <Star key={i} size={13} fill={i < r.rating ? "currentColor" : "none"} />
                  ))}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mb-1 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink-soft">{r.author}</p>
              <p className="mb-3 text-[14px] text-ink">{r.comment}</p>

              {r.reply || submittedIds.has(r.id) ? (
                <div className="rounded-control bg-paper-dim px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Your reply: </span>
                  {r.reply ?? replies[r.id]}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <Textarea
                    rows={2}
                    placeholder="Write a reply..."
                    value={replies[r.id] ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => handleReplyChange(r.id, e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <Button size="sm" className="self-start" disabled={!replies[r.id]?.trim()} onClick={() => handleSubmitReply(r.id)}>
                    Post reply
                  </Button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
