"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Input, Textarea, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function InstructorSettingsPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();

  const [bio, setBio] = useState("");
  const [portfolioUrl, setPortfolioUrl] = useState("");
  const [taxId, setTaxId] = useState("");
  const [emailNotifs, setEmailNotifs] = useState(true);
  const [qaNotifs, setQaNotifs] = useState(true);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your instructor settings." />;
  }

  function handleSave() {
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend instructor-settings mutation wired up yet.
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 3000);
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Instructor Settings</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Profile</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Full name" defaultValue={session.fullName} />
          <Textarea label="Instructor bio" rows={4} value={bio} onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)} placeholder="Tell learners about your background and expertise" />
          <Input label="Portfolio link" value={portfolioUrl} onChange={(e) => setPortfolioUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="GitHub, LinkedIn, or personal site" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Payout tax info</h2>
        <Input label="Tax ID / NTN (optional)" value={taxId} onChange={(e) => setTaxId(e.target.value)} />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Notification preferences</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Email me about new sales</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={emailNotifs}
              onChange={(e) => setEmailNotifs(e.target.checked)}
              aria-label="Toggle sales email notifications"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Email me about new Q&amp;A questions</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={qaNotifs}
              onChange={(e) => setQaNotifs(e.target.checked)}
              aria-label="Toggle Q&A email notifications"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Button size="lg" className="mt-6" onClick={handleSave}>
        {saved ? "Saved" : "Save changes"}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { MessageCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState, Badge } from "@saachi/ui";

type Student = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  courseTitle: string;
  progressPercent: number;
  lastActive: string;
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data. Replace with useInstructorStudents() once confirmed.
const STUDENTS: Student[] = [];

export default function InstructorStudentsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  const filtered = STUDENTS.filter((s) =>
    s.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || s.courseTitle.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Students</h1>
        <Input
          placeholder="Search students or courses..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="w-64"
        />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No students yet"
          description="Once learners enroll in your courses, they'll appear here."
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
            <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Student</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Course</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Progress</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Last active</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map((s) => (
                <tr key={s.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-medium text-ink">{s.name}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{s.courseTitle}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="h-1.5 w-24 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                        <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${s.progressPercent}%` }} />
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-ink-soft">{s.progressPercent}%</span>
                      {s.progressPercent === 100 && <Badge variant="success">Done</Badge>}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{s.lastActive}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                      <MessageCircle size={13} className="mr-1.5" />
                      Message
                    </Button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>

  );

FILE: apps\web\app\(instructor)\instructor\verification\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ShieldCheck, UploadCloud, Clock, CheckCircle2, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Input, Badge, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

type VerificationStatus = "not_submitted" | "pending" | "verified" | "rejected";

export default function InstructorVerificationPage() {
  // NOTE: demo-only local state. Replace with useInstructorVerification() once confirmed.
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<VerificationStatus>("not_submitted");
  const [idFileName, setIdFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [portfolioUrl, setPortfolioUrl] = useState("");
  const [introVideoUrl, setIntroVideoUrl] = useState("");

  function handleIdSelect(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) setIdFileName(file.name);
  }

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend verification-submission mutation wired up yet.
    setStatus("pending");
  }

  const STATUS_META: Record<VerificationStatus, { label: string; variant?: "gold" | "success" | "error"; icon: typeof Clock }> = {
    not_submitted: { label: "Not submitted", icon: ShieldCheck },
    pending: { label: "Pending review", variant: "gold", icon: Clock },
    verified: { label: "Verified", variant: "success", icon: CheckCircle2 },
    rejected: { label: "Rejected", variant: "error", icon: XCircle }
  };

  const meta = STATUS_META[status];
  const Icon = meta.icon;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg">
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Verification Status</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Get verified to unlock a lower commission tier and a trust badge.</p>

      <div
        className={cn(
          "mb-8 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-card border p-5",
          status === "verified" ? "border-success/30 bg-success-tint" :
          status === "rejected" ? "border-error/30 bg-error-tint" :
          status === "pending" ? "border-gold-dark/30 bg-gold-tint" :
          "border-line bg-card"
        )}
      >
        <Icon size={22} className="shrink-0 text-ink" />
        <div className="flex-1">
          <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Current status</p>
          {meta.variant ? (
            <Badge variant={meta.variant} className="mt-1">{meta.label}</Badge>
          ) : (
            <Badge className="mt-1">{meta.label}</Badge>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {(status === "not_submitted" || status === "rejected") && (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="mb-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-ink">Government ID or business registration</p>
            <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line px-4 py-3 text-[13px] text-ink-soft hover:border-gold-dark/40">
              <UploadCloud size={16} />
              {idFileName ?? "Upload ID document"}
              <input type="file" accept="image/*,.pdf" className="hidden" onChange={handleIdSelect} required />
            </label>
          </div>

          <Input
            label="Portfolio / proof-of-work link"
            required
            value={portfolioUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setPortfolioUrl(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="GitHub, LinkedIn, or past project samples"
          />

          <Input
            label="Intro video link (optional)"
            value={introVideoUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setIntroVideoUrl(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="A short video introducing yourself"
          />

          <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="self-start" disabled={!idFileName || !portfolioUrl.trim()}>
            Submit for verification
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}

      {status === "pending" && (
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Your submission is under review. This typically takes 2-3 business days.
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\become-instructor\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { GraduationCap } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState, Input, Textarea } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function BecomeInstructorPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [bio, setBio] = useState("");
  const [expertise, setExpertise] = useState("");
  const [portfolioUrl, setPortfolioUrl] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to apply as an instructor." />;
  }

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend application-review workflow wired up yet.
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSubmitting(false);
      setSubmitted(true);
    }, 600);
  }

  if (submitted) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg py-16">
        <EmptyState
          title="Application submitted"
          description="Our team will review your instructor application and get back to you by email."
          action={<Button onClick={() => router.push("/dashboard")}>Back to dashboard</Button>}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
      <h1 className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">
        <GraduationCap size={24} />
        Become an instructor
      </h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        Tell us about your expertise. Applications are reviewed manually before approval.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Textarea
          label="Bio"
          required
          rows={4}
          value={bio}
          onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Tell learners about your background and experience"
        />
        <Input
          label="Area of expertise"
          required
          value={expertise}
          onChange={(e) => setExpertise(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g. AI Automation & Workflow Tools"
        />
        <Input
          label="Portfolio link"
          value={portfolioUrl}
          onChange={(e) => setPortfolioUrl(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="GitHub, LinkedIn, or personal site"
        />
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" loading={submitting} className="self-start">
          Submit application
        </Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\cart\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useCartStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function CartPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const items = useCartStore((s) => s.items);
  const coupon = useCartStore((s) => s.coupon);
  const removeItem = useCartStore((s) => s.removeItem);
  const applyCoupon = useCartStore((s) => s.applyCoupon);
  const removeCoupon = useCartStore((s) => s.removeCoupon);
  const subtotal = useCartStore((s) => s.subtotal());
  const discount = useCartStore((s) => s.discount());
  const total = useCartStore((s) => s.total());

  const [couponCode, setCouponCode] = useState("");
  const [couponMessage, setCouponMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  function handleApplyCoupon() {
    const result = applyCoupon(couponCode);
    setCouponMessage(result.message);
  }

  const currency = items[0]?.currency ?? "USD";
  const fmt = (n: number) => new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency }).format(n);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Cart</h1>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Your cart is empty"
          description="Add a course to get started."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 md:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {items.map((item) => (
              <div key={item.courseId} className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4">
                {item.thumbnailUrl && (
                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                  <img
                    src={item.thumbnailUrl}
                    alt={item.title}
                    className="h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-control object-cover"
                  />
                )}
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:underline">
                    {item.title}
                  </Link>
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{item.instructorName}</p>
                </div>
                <span className="font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                  {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: item.currency }).format(item.price)}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label="Remove from cart"
                  onClick={() => removeItem(item.courseId)}
                  className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
                >
                  <X size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <aside className="h-fit rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Order summary</h2>

            {coupon ? (
              <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between rounded-control bg-gold-tint px-3 py-2 text-[13px]">
                <span className="font-semibold text-gold-dark">{coupon.code} applied</span>
                <button type="button" onClick={removeCoupon} className="text-ink-soft hover:text-ink">
                  Remove
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mb-4 flex gap-2">
                <Input
                  placeholder="Coupon code"
                  value={couponCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setCouponCode(e.target.value)}
                  className="flex-1"
                />
                <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleApplyCoupon}>
                  Apply
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
            {couponMessage && <p className="mb-4 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{couponMessage}</p>}

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-line pt-4 text-[14px]">
              <div className="flex justify-between text-ink-soft">
                <span>Subtotal</span>
                <span>{fmt(subtotal)}</span>
              </div>
              {discount > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between text-teal">
                  <span>Discount</span>
                  <span>-{fmt(discount)}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between border-t border-line pt-2 text-[16px] font-semibold text-ink">
                <span>Total</span>
                <span>{fmt(total)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <Button size="lg" className="mt-5 w-full" onClick={() => router.push("/checkout")}>
              Proceed to checkout
            </Button>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Award, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { useEnrollmentStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function CertificatesPage() {
  const enrollments = useEnrollmentStore((s) => s.enrollments);
  const completed = enrollments.filter((e) => e.progressPercent === 100);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Certificates</h1>

      {completed.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No certificates yet"
          description="Complete a course to earn your first certificate."
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {completed.map((e) => (
            <div key={e.courseId} className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-5">
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold-tint text-gold-dark">
                <Award size={22} />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <h3 className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{e.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Completed - {e.instructorName}</p>
              </div>
              <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                <Download size={14} className="mr-1.5" />
                Download
              </Button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { BookOpen, Award, Bell } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { useEnrollmentStore, useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, KpiCard } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function LearnerDashboardPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const enrollments = useEnrollmentStore((s) => s.enrollments);
  const notifications = useNotificationStore((s) => s.items);

  const inProgress = enrollments.filter((e) => e.progressPercent > 0 && e.progressPercent < 100);
  const completed = enrollments.filter((e) => e.progressPercent === 100);
  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">
        Welcome back{session ? `, ${session.fullName}` : ""}
      </h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">Here is where you left off.</p>

      <div className="mb-10 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <KpiCard label="Enrolled courses" value={String(enrollments.length)} icon={BookOpen} />
        <KpiCard label="Completed" value={String(completed.length)} icon={Award} />
        <KpiCard label="Unread notifications" value={String(unreadCount)} icon={Bell} />
      </div>

      <h2 className="mb-4 font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">Continue learning</h2>
      {inProgress.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No courses in progress"
          description="Enroll in a course to see it here."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {inProgress.map((e) => (
            <Link
              key={e.courseId}
              href={`/learn/${e.courseId}/lesson`}
              className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4 transition-colors hover:border-gold-dark/40"
            >
              {e.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={e.thumbnailUrl} alt={e.title} className="h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-control object-cover" />
              )}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <h3 className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{e.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{e.instructorName}</p>
                <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                  <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${e.progressPercent}%` }} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="shrink-0 text-[13px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">{e.progressPercent}%</span>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Video, CalendarDays } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type Session = {
  id: string;
  instructorName: string;
  topic: string;
  when: string;
  status: "upcoming" | "past";
};

const SESSIONS: Session[] = [
  { id: "s1", instructorName: "Ayesha Khan", topic: "n8n workflow review", when: "Mon, 10:00 AM", status: "upcoming" },
  { id: "s2", instructorName: "Hamza Malik", topic: "API integration Q&A", when: "Last Thu, 9:00 AM", status: "past" }

export default function MyMentorshipSessionsPage() {

  return (

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\mentorship\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { CalendarDays, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type MentorSlot = {
  id: string;
  instructorName: string;
  expertise: string;
  rate: string;
  slots: string[];
};

const MENTORS: MentorSlot[] = [
  {
    id: "m1",
    instructorName: "Ayesha Khan",
    expertise: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools",
    rate: "$40 / 30 min",
    slots: ["Mon 10:00 AM", "Wed 2:00 PM", "Fri 11:00 AM"]
  },
  {
    id: "m2",
    instructorName: "Hamza Malik",
    expertise: "AI Integration for Developers",
    rate: "$55 / 45 min",
    slots: ["Tue 4:00 PM", "Thu 9:00 AM"]
  }
];

export default function MentorshipBookingPage() {
  const [selectedMentor, setSelectedMentor] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedSlot, setSelectedSlot] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [booked, setBooked] = useState(false);

  const mentor = useMemo(() => MENTORS.find((m) => m.id === selectedMentor), [selectedMentor]);

  function handleBook() {
    if (!mentor || !selectedSlot) return;
    // NOTE: demo-only booking, no payment/calendar integration wired up yet.
    setBooked(true);
  }

  if (booked && mentor) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg py-16 text-center">
        <EmptyState
          title="Session booked"
          description={`Your session with ${mentor.instructorName} on ${selectedSlot} is confirmed. Check My Mentorship Sessions for the call link.`}
          action={
            <Link href="/mentorship/sessions">
              <Button>View my sessions</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Book mentorship</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">1-on-1 sessions with verified instructors.</p>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        {MENTORS.map((m) => (
          <Card
            key={m.id}
            className={`cursor-pointer p-5 transition-colors ${
              selectedMentor === m.id ? "border-gold-dark/60" : ""
            }`}
            onClick={() => {
              setSelectedMentor(m.id);
              setSelectedSlot(null);
            }}
          >
            <h3 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.instructorName}</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{m.expertise}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.rate}</p>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {mentor && (
        <div className="mt-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
            <CalendarDays size={16} />
            Available slots with {mentor.instructorName}
          </h2>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {mentor.slots.map((slot) => (
              <button
                key={slot}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedSlot(slot)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                  selectedSlot === slot
                    ? "border-gold-dark bg-gold-tint text-ink"
                    : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
                }`}
              >
                <Clock size={13} />
                {slot}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <Button className="mt-6" disabled={!selectedSlot} onClick={handleBook}>
            Confirm booking
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\my-courses\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useEnrollmentStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, Select } from "@saachi/ui";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "All", value: "all" },
  { label: "In progress", value: "in_progress" },
  { label: "Completed", value: "completed" },
  { label: "Not started", value: "not_started" }
];

export default function MyCoursesPage() {
  const enrollments = useEnrollmentStore((s) => s.enrollments);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");

  const filtered = enrollments.filter((e) => {
    if (status === "in_progress") return e.progressPercent > 0 && e.progressPercent < 100;
    if (status === "completed") return e.progressPercent === 100;
    if (status === "not_started") return e.progressPercent === 0;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">My Courses</h1>
        <Select options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} className="w-48" />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No courses here yet"
          description="Courses you enroll in will show up here."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {filtered.map((e) => (
            <Link
              key={e.courseId}
              href={`/learn/${e.courseId}/lesson`}
              className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card transition-colors hover:border-gold-dark/40"
            >
              {e.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={e.thumbnailUrl} alt={e.title} className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
              )}
              <div className="p-4">
                <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{e.title}</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{e.instructorName}</p>
                <div className="mt-3 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                  <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${e.progressPercent}%` }} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{e.progressPercent}% complete</p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Bell } from "lucide-react";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function NotificationsPage() {
  const items = useNotificationStore((s) => s.items);
  const markRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markRead);
  const markAllRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markAllRead);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Notifications</h1>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={markAllRead}>
          Mark all as read
        </Button>
      </div>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No notifications" description="You're all caught up." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {items.map((n) => (
            <button
              key={n.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => markRead(n.id)}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-start gap-3 rounded-card border border-line p-4 text-left transition-colors",
                n.read ? "bg-card" : "bg-gold-tint/40"
              )}
            >
              <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft">
                <Bell size={14} />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{n.title}</p>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{n.body}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft/60">
                  {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "short" }).format(
                    new Date(n.createdAt)
                  )}
                </p>
              </div>
              {!n.read && <span className="mt-1.5 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gold-dark" />}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\orders\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useOrderStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function OrderSuccessBanner() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const successId = searchParams.get("success");

  if (!successId) return null;

  return (
    <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-teal/30 bg-teal-light px-4 py-3">
      <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-teal" />
      <p className="text-[14px] text-ink">
        Payment successful. Order <span className="font-semibold">{successId}</span> is confirmed - your
        courses are now available in My Courses.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function OrdersTable() {
  const orders = useOrderStore((s) => s.orders);

  if (orders.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState title="No orders yet" description="Your purchase history will show up here." />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
        <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
          <tr>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Order</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Items</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Total</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Date</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {orders.map((order) => (
            <tr key={order.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-medium text-ink">{order.id}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{order.items.map((i) => i.title).join(", ")}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-mono text-ink">
                {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: order.currency }).format(order.total)}
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 capitalize text-ink-soft">{order.status}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">
                {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { dateStyle: "medium" }).format(new Date(order.createdAt))}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function OrdersPage() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Order History</h1>

      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <OrderSuccessBanner />
      </Suspense>

      <OrdersTable />
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Download, ShieldAlert, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsPrivacyPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [cookiePrefs, setCookiePrefs] = useState({ analytics: true, marketing: false });
  const [exportRequested, setExportRequested] = useState(false);

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your privacy settings." />;
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Privacy</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Cookie preferences</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Analytics cookies</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={cookiePrefs.analytics}
              onChange={(e) => setCookiePrefs((p) => ({ ...p, analytics: e.target.checked }))}
              aria-label="Toggle analytics cookies"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Marketing cookies</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={cookiePrefs.marketing}
              onChange={(e) => setCookiePrefs((p) => ({ ...p, marketing: e.target.checked }))}
              aria-label="Toggle marketing cookies"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <Download size={16} />
          Data export
        </h2>
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Request a copy of the personal data associated with your account.
        </p>
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={exportRequested} onClick={() => setExportRequested(true)}>
          {exportRequested ? "Export requested" : "Request data export"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-error/30 bg-error-tint p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <ShieldAlert size={16} />
          Delete account
        </h2>
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Permanently delete your account and all associated data. This cannot be undone.
        </p>
        <Button variant="danger">
          <Trash2 size={14} className="mr-1.5" />
          Request account deletion
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\settings\security\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ShieldCheck, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsSecurityPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [twoFactorEnabled, setTwoFactorEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your security settings." />;
  }

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend auth mutation wired up yet.
    setCurrentPassword("");
    setNewPassword("");
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Security</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <ShieldCheck size={16} />
          Change password
        </h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input
            label="Current password"
            type="password"
            value={currentPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Input
            label="New password"
            type="password"
            value={newPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Button type="submit" className="self-start">
            Update password
          </Button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Smartphone size={16} className="text-ink-soft" />
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Two-factor authentication</p>
              <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Add an extra layer of security to your account.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={twoFactorEnabled}
            onChange={(e) => setTwoFactorEnabled(e.target.checked)}
            aria-label="Toggle two-factor authentication"
            className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Active sessions</h2>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">This device - {session.email}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, Input, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your settings." />;
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Settings</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Profile</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Full name" defaultValue={session.fullName} />
          <Input label="Email" defaultValue={session.email} type="email" />
          <Button className="self-start">Save changes</Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Session</h2>
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Logged in as {session.email}</p>
        <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => logout.mutate()}>
          Log out
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useCartStore, useWishlistStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function WishlistPage() {
  const items = useWishlistStore((s) => s.items);
  const removeFromWishlist = useWishlistStore((s) => s.remove);
  const addToCart = useCartStore((s) => s.addItem);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Wishlist</h1>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Your wishlist is empty"
          description="Save courses you're interested in to find them here later."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.courseId}
              className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4"
            >
              {item.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img
                  src={item.thumbnailUrl}
                  alt={item.title}
                  className="h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-control object-cover"
                />
              )}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:underline">
                  {item.title}
                </Link>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{item.instructorName}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: item.currency }).format(item.price)}
              </span>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                onClick={() =>
                  addToCart({
                    courseId: item.courseId,
                    slug: item.slug,
                    title: item.title,
                    thumbnailUrl: item.thumbnailUrl,
                    instructorName: item.instructorName,
                    price: item.price,
                    currency: item.currency
                  })
                }
              >
                Add to cart
              </Button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Remove from wishlist"
                onClick={() => removeFromWishlist(item.courseId)}
                className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
              >
                <X size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Button, Input, Textarea, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function ContactPage() {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend yet. Replace with a real Support/Contact API call.
    setSubmitted(true);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-2 font-display text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">Contact us</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[15px] text-ink-soft">
        Questions about courses, instructor applications, or anything else - send us a message.
      </p>

      {submitted ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Message sent"
          description="Thanks for reaching out. We typically respond within 1-2 business days."
        />
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input label="Your name" required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          <Input
            label="Email"
            type="email"
            required
            value={email}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Textarea
            label="Message"
            required
            rows={6}
            value={message}
            onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="How can we help?"
          />
          <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="self-start">
            Send message
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="mt-10 border-t border-line pt-6 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        <p>Support email: support@saachi.ai</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\courses\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useCourseList, type CourseListFilters } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CourseCard, EmptyState, FilterSidebar, Input, Select, Loading, type FilterSidebarValue } from "@saachi/ui";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../../lib/categories";

const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Most popular", value: "popular" },
  { label: "Newest", value: "newest" },
  { label: "Price: low to high", value: "price_asc" },
  { label: "Price: high to low", value: "price_desc" },
  { label: "Highest rated", value: "rating" }
];

const LEVEL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Beginner", value: "beginner" },
  { label: "Intermediate", value: "intermediate" },
  { label: "Advanced", value: "advanced" }
];

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "English", value: "en" },
  { label: "Urdu", value: "ur" }
];

export default function CoursesPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState("popular");
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FilterSidebarValue>({ categories: [], level: undefined, language: undefined });

  const queryFilters: CourseListFilters = useMemo(
    () => ({
      search: search || undefined,
      category: filters.categories[0],
      level: filters.level,
      language: filters.language,
      sort
    }),
    [search, filters, sort]
  );

  const { data, isLoading } = useCourseList(queryFilters);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 md:px-8">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[28px] font-bold text-ink">Course Marketplace</h1>

      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
        <Input
          placeholder="Search courses or instructors..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="md:max-w-sm"
        />
        <Select
          options={SORT_OPTIONS}
          value={sort}
          onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)}
          className="md:w-56"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 md:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
        <FilterSidebar
          categoryOptions={CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id }))}
          levelOptions={LEVEL_OPTIONS}
          languageOptions={LANGUAGE_OPTIONS}
          value={filters}
          onChange={setFilters}
        />

        <div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <Loading label="Loading courses..." />
          ) : !data || data.items.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState title="No courses found" description="Try adjusting your filters or search term." />
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{data.total} courses found</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {data.items.map((course) => (
                  <CourseCard
                    key={course.id}
                    slug={course.slug}
                    title={course.title}
                    thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                    instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                    price={course.price}
                    currency={course.currency}
                    rating={course.rating}
                    ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\search\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Suspense, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search as SearchIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useCourseList } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CourseCard, EmptyState, Input, Loading } from "@saachi/ui";

function SearchResults() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const initialQuery = searchParams.get("q") ?? "";
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(initialQuery);

  const { data, isLoading } = useCourseList({ search: initialQuery || undefined });

  function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    router.push(`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`);
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 md:px-8">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Search</h1>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mb-8 flex max-w-lg gap-2">
        <Input
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search courses or instructors..."
          className="flex-1"
        />
      </form>

      {!initialQuery ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Search Saachi AI"
          description="Type a course, topic, or instructor name above to get started."
        />
      ) : isLoading ? (
        <Loading label="Searching..." />
      ) : !data || data.items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title={`No results for "${initialQuery}"`}
          description="Try a different search term or browse categories instead."
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <p className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
            <SearchIcon size={14} />
            {data.total} results for &quot;{initialQuery}&quot;
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
            {data.items.map((course) => (
              <CourseCard
                key={course.id}
                slug={course.slug}
                title={course.title}
                thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                price={course.price}
                currency={course.currency}
                rating={course.rating}
                ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SearchPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<Loading label="Loading..." />}>
      <SearchResults />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCourseList } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, CourseCard, Loading } from "@saachi/ui";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../lib/categories";

export default function HomePage() {
  const { data: featured, isLoading } = useCourseList({ sort: "popular", page: 1 });

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Hero */}
      <section className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 pb-14 pt-16 md:px-8 md:pt-24">
        <div className="max-w-2xl">
          <h1 className="font-display text-[38px] font-bold leading-[1.1] text-ink md:text-[52px]">
            Learn practical AI skills. Sell what you know.
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-5 text-[16px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft md:text-[18px]">
            Not another watch-time platform. Saachi AI is a skills exchange for the AI era Ã¢â‚¬â€
            structured, practical, and paid.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button size="lg">Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
            <Link href="/register">
              <Button size="lg" variant="secondary">
                Become an instructor
              </Button>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Category grid */}
      <section className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 pb-16 md:px-8">
        <h2 className="mb-5 font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Browse by category</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">
          {CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
            <Link
              key={cat.id}
              href={`/categories/${cat.slug}`}
              className="rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4 text-[14px] font-medium text-ink transition-colors hover:border-gold-dark/40"
            >
              {cat.name}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Featured courses */}
      <section className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 pb-20 md:px-8">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Featured courses</h2>
          <Link href="/courses" className="text-[14px] font-medium text-gold-dark hover:underline">
            View all
          </Link>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <Loading label="Loading courses..." />
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
            {featured?.items.map((course) => (
              <CourseCard
                key={course.id}
                slug={course.slug}
                title={course.title}
                thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                price={course.price}
                currency={course.currency}
                rating={course.rating}
                ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\_components\SiteHeader.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { ShoppingCart, Heart, Menu, X, LogOut } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, ThemeToggle } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useCartStore, useWishlistStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";

const NAV_LINKS = [
  { href: "/courses", label: "Courses" },
  { href: "/categories/ai-automation", label: "Automation" },
  { href: "/categories/ai-marketing", label: "Marketing" },
  { href: "/about", label: "About" }
];

export function SiteHeader() {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const cartCount = useCartStore((s) => s.items.length);
  const wishlistCount = useWishlistStore((s) => s.items.length);
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-line bg-card/90 backdrop-blur">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 md:px-8">
        <Link href="/" className="font-display text-[20px] font-bold text-ink">
          Saachi<span className="text-gold-dark">AI</span>
        </Link>

        <nav className="hidden items-center gap-6 md:flex">
          {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
            <Link
              key={link.href}
              href={link.href}
              className="text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:text-ink"
            >
              {link.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <ThemeToggle />

          <Link
            href="/wishlist"
            aria-label="Wishlist"
            className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <Heart size={18} />
            {wishlistCount > 0 && (
              <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">
                {wishlistCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/cart"
            aria-label="Cart"
            className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <ShoppingCart size={18} />
            {cartCount > 0 && (
              <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">
                {cartCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>

          {session ? (
            <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
              <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{session.fullName}</span>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="ghost"
                onClick={() => logout.mutate()}
                aria-label="Log out"
              >
                <LogOut size={14} />
              </Button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
              <Link href="/login">
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                  Log in
                </Button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/register">
                <Button size="sm">Sign up</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft md:hidden"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen((v) => !v)}
            aria-label="Toggle menu"
          >
            {mobileOpen ? <X size={20} /> : <Menu size={20} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="border-t border-line bg-card px-4 py-4 md:hidden">
          <nav className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              <Link
                key={link.href}
                href={link.href}
                onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                className="text-[15px] font-medium text-ink"
              >
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
            <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
              {session ? (
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => logout.mutate()} className="w-full">
                  Log out
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Link href="/login" className="flex-1">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="w-full">
                      Log in
                    </Button>
                  </Link>
                  <Link href="/register" className="flex-1">
                    <Button size="sm" className="w-full">
                      Sign up
                    </Button>
                  </Link>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </nav>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\error.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Button } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function GlobalError({
  error,
  reset
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error(error);
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-lg flex-col items-center px-4 py-24 text-center md:px-0">
      <span className="font-mono text-[64px] font-bold text-error">500</span>
      <h1 className="mt-2 font-display text-[24px] font-bold text-ink">Something went wrong</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.
      </p>
      <div className="mt-8 flex gap-3">
        <Button onClick={() => reset()}>Try again</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\providers.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";

export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [client] = useState(() => new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: 30_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } }
  }));

  return <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
}

FILE: apps\web\lib\categories.ts

export type CategoryDef = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
};

export const CATEGORIES: CategoryDef[] = [
  { id: "ai-fundamentals", name: "AI Fundamentals & Prompt Engineering", slug: "ai-fundamentals" },
  { id: "ai-marketing", name: "AI for Marketing & Sales", slug: "ai-marketing" },
  { id: "ai-automation", name: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools", slug: "ai-automation" },
  { id: "ai-support", name: "AI Customer Support & Communication", slug: "ai-support" },
  { id: "ai-dev", name: "AI Website & App Development", slug: "ai-dev" },
  { id: "ai-integration", name: "AI Integration for Developers", slug: "ai-integration" },
  { id: "ai-design", name: "AI Design & Creative Tools", slug: "ai-design" },
  { id: "ai-career", name: "AI for Careers & Job Search", slug: "ai-career" }
];

export function getCategoryBySlug(slug: string): CategoryDef | undefined {
  return CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.slug === slug);
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\middleware.ts
================================================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

const LEARNER_PREFIX = "/dashboard";
const INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX = "/instructor";

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const sessionCookie = request.cookies.get("saachi_session");

  const isProtected =
    pathname.startsWith(LEARNER_PREFIX) || pathname.startsWith(INSTRUCTOR_PREFIX);

  if (isProtected && !sessionCookie) {
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    loginUrl.searchParams.set("redirect", pathname);
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/dashboard/:path*", "/instructor/:path*"]
};

  reactStrictMode: true,

    ignoreBuildErrors: false // re-enabled by Fix-SAI2-Issues.ps1
  },

    ignoreDuringBuilds: false // re-enabled by Fix-SAI2-Issues.ps1
  },
  images: {
    remotePatterns: [
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "**.cloudfront.net" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "res.cloudinary.com" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "picsum.photos" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "images.unsplash.com" },
      { protocol: "https", hostname: "placehold.co" }

    return [

  }
};

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminCourses.ts

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES,
  type AdminCourse,
  type AdminCourseStatus,
  type AdminCategory
} from "../dummy-data-admin-courses";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Admin-Courses / Categories API (Doc 3).
let COURSES_STATE: AdminCourse[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_COURSES];
let CATEGORIES_STATE: AdminCategory[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_CATEGORIES];

export const adminCourseKeys = {
  all: ["admin", "courses"] as const,
  moderationQueue: ["admin", "courses", "moderation-queue"] as const,
  catalog: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "courses", "catalog", filters] as const,
  categories: ["admin", "categories"] as const
};

export function useAdminModerationQueue() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.moderationQueue,
    queryFn: async () => delay(COURSES_STATE.filter((c) => c.status === "pending_review"))
  });
}

export type AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {
  search?: string;
  categoryId?: string;
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminCourseCatalog(filters: AdminCourseCatalogFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.catalog(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...COURSES_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter((c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.instructorName.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      }
      if (filters.categoryId) items = items.filter((c) => c.categoryId === filters.categoryId);
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((c) => c.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useModerateCourse() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({
      id,
      decision,
      reason
    }: {
      id: string;
      decision: "published" | "rejected";
      reason?: string;
    }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) =>
        c.id === id ? { ...c, status: decision as AdminCourseStatus, rejectionReason: reason } : c
      );
      return delay({ id, decision }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useToggleCourseFlag() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, field }: { id: string; field: "featured" | "pinned" }) => {
      COURSES_STATE = COURSES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, [field]: !c[field] } : c));
      return delay({ id, field }, 200);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminCategoryList() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...CATEGORIES_STATE])
  });
}

export function useCreateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      const slug = input.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");
      const created: AdminCategory = { id: slug, name: input.name, slug, courseCount: 0, tags: input.tags };
      CATEGORIES_STATE = [...CATEGORIES_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdateCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, name, tags }: { id: string; name: string; tags: string[] }) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, name, tags } : c));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

export function useDeleteCategory() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) => {
      CATEGORIES_STATE = CATEGORIES_STATE.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminCourseKeys.categories });
    }
  });
}

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TRANSACTIONS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_PAYOUTS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_REFUNDS,
  ADMIN_DUMMY_TAX_RULES,
  type AdminTransaction,
  type AdminPayout,
  type PayoutStatus,
  type AdminRefund,
  type RefundStatus,
  type TaxRule
} from "../dummy-data-admin-finance";

function delay<T>(value: T, ms = 350): Promise<T> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), ms));
}

// NOTE: demo-only, in-memory. Replace with real Finance/Payouts/Refunds/Tax API (Doc 3).
let TRANSACTIONS_STATE: AdminTransaction[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TRANSACTIONS];
let PAYOUTS_STATE: AdminPayout[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_PAYOUTS];
let REFUNDS_STATE: AdminRefund[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_REFUNDS];
let TAX_RULES_STATE: TaxRule[] = [...ADMIN_DUMMY_TAX_RULES];

export const adminFinanceKeys = {
  all: ["admin", "finance"] as const,
  transactions: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "transactions", filters] as const,
  payouts: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "payouts", filters] as const,
  refunds: (filters?: Record<string, unknown>) => ["admin", "finance", "refunds", filters] as const,
  taxRules: ["admin", "finance", "tax-rules"] as const
};

export type TransactionFilters = {
  search?: string;
  status?: string;
  gateway?: string;
};

export function useAdminTransactions(filters: TransactionFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.transactions(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...TRANSACTIONS_STATE];
      if (filters.search) {
        const q = filters.search.toLowerCase();
        items = items.filter(
          (t) =>
            t.courseTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.learnerName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.instructorName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            t.id.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((t) => t.status === filters.status);
      if (filters.gateway) items = items.filter((t) => t.gateway === filters.gateway);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export type PayoutFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminPayouts(filters: PayoutFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.payouts(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...PAYOUTS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((p) => p.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useUpdatePayoutStatus() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: PayoutStatus }) => {
      PAYOUTS_STATE = PAYOUTS_STATE.map((p) =>
        p.id === id
          ? { ...p, status, processedAt: status === "paid" ? new Date().toISOString() : p.processedAt }
          : p
      );
      return delay({ id, status }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export type RefundFilters = {
  status?: string;
};

export function useAdminRefunds(filters: RefundFilters = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.refunds(filters),
    queryFn: async () => {
      let items = [...REFUNDS_STATE];
      if (filters.status) items = items.filter((r) => r.status === filters.status);
      return delay(items);
    }
  });
}

export function useResolveRefund() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, status }: { id: string; status: RefundStatus }) => {
      REFUNDS_STATE = REFUNDS_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, status } : r));
      if (status === "approved") {
        const refund = REFUNDS_STATE.find((r) => r.id === id);
        if (refund) {
          TRANSACTIONS_STATE = TRANSACTIONS_STATE.map((t) =>
            t.id === refund.transactionId ? { ...t, status: "refunded" as const } : t
          );
        }
      }
      return delay({ id, status }, 400);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.all });
    }
  });
}

export function useAdminTaxRules() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules,
    queryFn: async () => delay([...TAX_RULES_STATE])
  });
}

export function useUpdateTaxRule() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, ratePercent, active }: { id: string; ratePercent: number; active: boolean }) => {
      TAX_RULES_STATE = TAX_RULES_STATE.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ratePercent, active } : r));
      return delay({ id }, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules });
    }
  });
}

export function useCreateTaxRule() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { region: string; ratePercent: number; appliesTo: string }) => {
      const created: TaxRule = {
        id: `tax_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        region: input.region,
        ratePercent: input.ratePercent,
        appliesTo: input.appliesTo,
        active: true
      };
      TAX_RULES_STATE = [...TAX_RULES_STATE, created];
      return delay(created, 300);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: adminFinanceKeys.taxRules });
    }
  });
}

FILE: packages\api-client\src\hooks\useAdminMarketing.ts
